       1. kuṟaḷ, 674.

   tām aṟamuṇarnta varalāṟu kūṟal
   aṟapporuḷ viḷaikkuṅ kāṭci yaruntava raruḷiṟ ṟaṉṟip
       piṟappuṇarn tataṉiṉ yāmē peyarttuṇarn tiṭavum paṭṭa
       tiṟappavu mataṉkaṇ tēṟṟam iṉituvait tiṭumi ṉeṉṟāṉ
       uṟappaṇit tevaru muḷḷat tuvantaṉar kēṭka luṟṟār.         73

   urai:- aṟapporuḷ viḷaikkum kāṭci-yāṉ kūṟum aṟamākiya poruḷ viḷaivikkum
   payaṉai, aruntavar aruḷiṟṟaṉṟi-ariya tavattaiyuṭaiya muṉivararkaḷ
   uraittaruḷiyataṉālu maṉṟi, piṟappuṇarntataṉiṉ-paḻam piṟappu
   varalāṟukaḷai yaṟiyu maṟivuṭaimaiyāl, yāmē- emmāl, peyarttum
   uṇarntiṭappaṭṭatu-maṟupaṭiyum uṇarappaṭṭatākum, ataṉkaṇ-ataṉāl avvaṟap
   poruḷiṉkaṇ, tēṟṟam - karuttai,iṟappavum - mikavum teḷivāka, iṉitu
   vaittiṭumiṉ - iṉitu vaittuk kēṭpīrāka, eṉṟāṉ - eṉṟu apayaruci
   kūṟiṉāṉāka, evarum - āṅkirunta yāvarum, uṟappaṇintu - mikavum paṇintu,
   uvantaṉar kēṭkaluṟṟār - viruppattuṭaṉ kēṭpārāyiṉar e-ṟu.
   aṟamākiya poruḷāl viḷaiyum payaṉ karuvikaḷāl kāṇappaṭutaliṉ kāṭci
   yeṉappaṭṭatu. kōḻiyāyirunta kālattu akampaṉa muṉikaḷum, iraṭṭaiyarāyp
   piṟantapiṉ cutatta muṉikaḷum paḻampiṟappiṉai yuṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ,
   "aruntavar" eṉṟāṉ. tiruvaṟam uṇarntu tiruntiya aṟiviṉa rāṉapōtu
   tāmākavum uṇarntu koṇṭatu eṉpāṉ, "peyart tuṇarntiṭavum paṭṭatu" eṉṟāṉ.
   ummai pirittukkūṭṭappaṭṭatu. iṟappavum iṉitu eṉpaṉa vaṉpuṟai kuṟittu
   niṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu upatēca mukattālum kāṭci vakaiyālum uṇarntatu eṉavē,
   yāvarkkum apporuḷaik kēṭṭaṟku nāṭṭa muṇṭāyiṟṟeṉpār, "uṟappaṇintu evarum
   uḷḷat tuvantaṉar kēṭkaluṟṟār" eṉṟār. uvantaṉar, muṟṟeccam. aracaṉ mutal
   āṅkirunta makkaḷaṉaivarum eṉṟaṟku "evarum" eṉṟār.
   mutaṟ carukkam muṟṟiṟṟu.
   ---------------

                          iraṇṭāvatu carukkam

   ippakutikkaṇ, avantināṭṭu uñcayiṉi nakarattē acōkaṉ eṉṉum vēntaṉ
   cantiramatiyeṉpāḷai maṇantu yacōtaraṉeṉṉum makaṉaippeṟṟu iṉitiruppatum,
   avaṉ orukāl taṉakku naraiyuṇṭāṉatu kaṇṭu varunti, yacōtaraṉai
   aracaṉākkiviṭut tavamēṟkoḷvāṉ kuṇataraṉeṉṉum muṉivaṉpāl aṟaṅkēṭṭut
   tavampurintiruppatum, yacōtaraṉ, aṟattai nekiḻttup poruḷiṉpaṅkaḷil
   tōyntu kiṭappatum, avaṟkum avaṉ maṉaivi amirtamatiyeṉpāṭkum
   yacōmatiyeṉṉum makaṉ piṟantiruppatum, cila yāṇṭukaṭkuppiṉ orunāḷ
   amirtamati kaṇavaṉōṭu paḷḷiyil kaṇṇuṟaṅkupavaḷ, aṭṭa paṅkaṉeṉṉum
   yāṉaippākaṉoruvaṉ māḷavapañcamam eṉṉum paṇṇaip pāṭakkēṭṭu maṉamuruki
   avaṉpāṟ kaḷḷak kāmaṅ koḷvatum, kuṇavati eṉṉum tōḻi palavāṟu eṭuttōti
   avaḷ ceyalai vilakkavum, amirtamati ēlātu, aṭṭapaṅkaṉaik
   kūṭṭuvikkavēṇṭumeṉa ak kuṇavatiyai vaṟpuṟuttuvatum, avaḷ icaintuceṉṟu
   aṭṭapaṅkaṉai matiyuṭampaṭuppatum, amirtamatiyum aṭṭapaṅkaṉum kaḷḷakkāma
   nukarcci peṟṟuvaruvatum, avaṭku yacōtaraṉpāl muṉivu muṟukuvatum, aracaṉ
   aiyuṟṟu avaḷatu kaḷḷavoḻukkattai yaṟintu mutaṟkaṇ kolai puriya niṉaintu
   piṉ māṟuvatum, aracavaikkaṇ amirtamatiyuṭaṉ acatiyāṭikkuṟippuraiyāl
   ikaḻvatum, taṉakkuḷḷē makaḷirpōkamum araciyaṟpōkamum veṟuttu,
   amirtamatiyiṉ kaḷḷavoḻukkattai oru kaṉaviṉmēl vaittut tāyākiya
   cantiramatikku uraippatum, avaḷ caṇṭamārikku uyirppali tantu vaḻipaṭṭuk
   kaṉavukāṭṭiya tīṅku oḻika eṉpatum, avaṉ uṭaṉpaṭātoḻivatum, avaḷ ciṉantu
   māvāl kōḻiyoṉṟu ceytu paliyiṭṭuvaḻipaṭuka veṉpatum, avaṉ avvāṟē
   ceytapōtu marakkōḻi uyiruṭaiyatupōl kūvi viḻutalum, avaṉ varuntit
   tuṟavu pūṇpatum, amirtamati avaṉukkum cantiramatikkum nañcuṇavutantu
   kolvatum, iruvarum vilaṅkukaḷāyp piṟappatum, yacōmati aracaṉātalum
   piṟavum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   -----------
   avantināṭṭu uñcayiṉi nakaram
   vaḷavayal vāriyiṉ malinta palpati
       aḷavaṟu caṉapata mavanti yāmatiṉ
       viḷaipaya ṉamararum virumpu nīrmaiya
       tuḷatoru nakaratuñ cayiṉi yeṉpavē.         74

   urai: vaḷavayal vāriyiṉ - vaḷaviya vayalkaḷākiya varuvāyāl, malinta -
   celvamniṟainta, palpati - palanakaraṅkaṭku iṭamākiya, aḷavaṟu caṉa
   patam - aḷavillāta makkaḷaiyuṭaiya nāṭu, avantiyām - avantināṭākum,
   atiṉ viḷaipayaṉ - ataṉkaṇ vāḻvārk keytum pōkam, amararum virumpum
   nīrmaiyatu - tēvarum virumpattakka taṉmaittākum, orunakar uḷatu -
   annāṭṭiṟkut talaiyāya nakar oṉṟu uṇṭu, atu uñcayiṉi eṉpa - ataṉai
   uñcayiṉi eṉṟu periyōr kūṟuvārkaḷ. e - ṟu.
   vayalvaḷattāl mikka viḷaiporuḷum, ataṉ vāyilākap peruñcelvamum
   perukutaliṉ, vaḷavayalai “vāri” yeṉṟār. vāri, varuvāy; “puyaleṉṉum
   vāri” (14) eṉṟa tirukkuṟaṭkup parimēlaḻakiyār kūṟum urai kāṇka.
   caṉapatam, nāṭu; ummai, ciṟappu. aḵteṉṉal vēṇṭum āytam,
   vikārattāltokkatu. uñcayiṉi, vaṭacoṟcitaivu. eṉpa veṉṟataṉai
   acaiyākkiṉumamaiyum.
   ----------
   nāṭṭaracaṉ acōkaṉ eṉal
   kantaṭu kaḷimata yāṉai maṉṉavaṉ
       intira ṉeṉuntiṟa lacōka ṉeṉṟuḷaṉ
       cantira matiyeṉṉum maṭantai taṉṉuṭaṉ
       antami luvakaiya ṉamarntu vaikunāḷ.         75

   urai: kantuaṭu kaḷimata yāṉai - kaṭṭuttaṟiyai alaikkum
   matakkaḷippiṉaiyuṭaiya yāṉaikaḷaiyuṭaiya, maṉṉavaṉ. aracaṉāvāṉ, intiraṉ
   eṉum tiṟal - tuṟakkavēntaṉāṉa intiraṉēyeṉṟu collattakka tiṟalpaṭaitta,
   acōkaṉ eṉṟu uḷaṉ - acōkaṉ eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭuvāṉ uḷaṉ, cantiramati
   eṉum maṭantai taṉṉuṭaṉ - cantiramatiyeṉṉum maṅkainallāḷuṭaṉkūṭi, antam
   il uvakaiyil-muṭivillāta iṉpattaiyuṭaiyaṉāy, amarntu vaikumnāḷ - ataṉai
   virumpivāḻum nāṭkaḷil e-ṟu.
   vaṟitēyuḷḷa yāṉai kantiṉai alaippataṟkuk kāraṇam matakkaḷippuṭaimai
   yeṉṟaṟku, "kantaṭu kaḷimatayāṉai" eṉṟār. poḻikiṉṟa matamuṭaimai
   tōṉṟaṟku, "kaḷimatayāṉai" eṉal vēṇṭiṟṟu. pōkappēṟṟālēyaṉṟi valiyālum
   intiraṉai yoppāṉ eṉpār, "intiraṉeṉun tiṟal acōkaṉ" eṉṟār. maṭantai,
   paruvattālum potuviyalpālum kūṟiyatu. cantiramatiyiṉ kūṭṭam kūṭuntōṟum
   puttiṉpam payantuvantamaiyiṉ, "antamil uvakaiyaṉ" eṉṟār. antamiṉmai
   amarntu vaikaṟku ētu.
   -------------
   yacōtaraṉ piṟappu
   intuvō riḷampiṟai payanta teṉṉavē
       cantira matiyoru taṉayaṉ tantaṉaḷ
       entuyar kaḷaipava ṉicō taraṉṉeṉa
       nantiya pukaḻavaṉ nāma mōtiṉāṉ.         76

   urai:-intu - maḻumatiyāṉatu, ōr iḷampiṟai payanta teṉṉa - ōr
   piṟaiccantiraṉaip peṟṟāṟpōla, cantiramati - cantiramatiyākiya araci,
   oru taṉayaṉ tantaṉaḷ - oru makaṉaip peṟṟāḷ, em tuyar kaḷaipavaṉ - eṅkaḷ
   tuṉpattait tuṭaippavaṉāṉa acōkamaṉṉaṉ, nantiya pukaḻ avaṉ -
   mikkapukaḻaiyuṭaiya anta makaṉukku, icōtaraṉ eṉa - yacatōraṉṉeṉṟu,
   nāmam ōtiṉāṉ - peyar vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
   cantiramati yacōtaraṉaippeṟṟatu, muḻumati iḷampiṟaiyai īṉṟatupōla
   eṉṟār, cantiramatiyiṉ aṟivē yacōtaraṉ matiyait taṉvaḻi yoḻukuvitta
   tātaliṉ. muḻumati iḷamatiyaiyīṉal, ilporuḷuvamai. emmaippōl
   piṟavittuyarkaḷaiyum nalloḻukkamuṭaiyaṉeṉpāṉ,"emtuyar kaḷaipava" eṉṟu
   apayaruci kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. em tuyar kaḷaipavaṉ eṉṟatai yacōtaraṟkē yēṟṟi,
   tuyarkaḷaipavaṉātalāl yacōtaraṉeṉap peyariṭṭāṉ eṉṟumām.
   --------------
   yacōtaraṉ iḷavaracaṉāy amirtamatiyeṉpāḷai maṇantu makiḻtal
   iḷaṅkaḷiṟ ṟuḻuvaiyi* ṉēta miṉṟiyē
       vaḷaṅkeḻu kumaraṉum vaḷarntu maṉṉaṉāy
       viḷaṅkiḻai yamiḻtamuṉ matiyai vēḷviyāṉ
       uḷaṅkoḷap puṇarntuṭa ṉuvakai yeytiṉāṉ.         77
       ---------
       (pāṭam)* iḷaṅkaḷiṟuḻuvaiyiṉ, iḷaṅkaḷiṟṟoruttaliṉ.

   urai:- iḷaṅkaḷiṟṟu uḻuvaiyiṉ āṇpulikkuruḷai pōla. ētam iṉṟi - nalivu
   ciṟitum iṉṟi, vaḷamkeḻu kumaraṉum - valiporuntiya yacōtaraṉum, vaḷarntu
   - vaḷarccipeṟṟu, maṉṉaṉāy - iḷavaracaṉāki, viḷaṅkuiḻai amiḻtamuṉmatiyai
   - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyuṭaiya amirtamatiyeṉpavaḷai, vēḷviyāṉ -
   tirumaṇattāl, uḷaṅkoḷa - maṉam iṉpamniṟaiya, puṇarntu - kūṭi, uṭaṉ
   uvakai eytiṉāṉ - uṭaṉeytum iṉpattai nukarntu varalāṉāṉ e-ṟu.
   kaḷiṟu, vilaṅkiṉ āṇpāṟpeyer; “ēṟum ēṟṟaiyum oruttalum kaḷiṟum”1 eṉa
   āciriyar kūṟutal kāṇka. ik kaḷiṟeṉṉuñ col vēḻam, kēḻal eṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum
   ciṟappaveytuvippavāyiṉum, īṇṭu itu potuvitiyāṉ amaintateṉavuṇarka.
   “iḷaṅkaḷi ṟuḻuvaiyiṉ ētamiṉṟiyē”eṉṟu pāṭaṅ koḷḷiṉ, puliyāl tīṅkiṉṟi
   iḷaṅkaḷiṟu vaḷarntāṟpōla eṉṟu poruḷkoḷka. acōkaṉāṭciyilātal,
   yacōtaraṉāṭci yilātal uḻuvai pōlum pakaiyuṇmai kūṟappaṭāmaiyiṉ, atu
   pāṭamaṉmaiyaṟika. vaḷam, īṇṭu meyvaṉmaimēṟṟu. “maṉṉaṉāy” eṉṟu
   īṇṭukkūṟiyatu, piṟāṇṭu, “yacōtaraṉṉeṉun taṉayaṉai
   nilamakaṭṭalaivaṉākeṉa” (83) muṭicūṭṭal kūṟappaṭutaliṉ,
   iḷavaracaṉātalaikkuṟittu niṟpatāyiṟṟu. puṇarcci nikaḻumiṭattē iṉpamum
   uṭaṉnikaḻtalpaṟṟi, “uṭaṉ uvakaiyeytiṉāṉ” eṉṟār. puṇarcciyiṉpamē neñcil
   eññāṉṟum nilavap puṇarntaṉareṉṟaṟku, "uḷaṅkoḷap puṇarntu" eṉac
   ciṟappittār; itaṉāṟpayaṉ, nallaṟivukāṭṭum naṉṉeṟiyaik kaṭaipōkac cellum
   kaṭaippiṭi avaṉpāl iṉmaiyuṇarttuvatu.
   --------
       1.tol.po.557

   yacōtaraṉukku yacōmatiyeṉṉum makaṉ piṟattal
   iḷaiyavaḷ eḻilnalam ēntu koṅkaiyiṉ
       viḷaipaya ṉicōtaraṉ viḻaintu cellunāḷ*
       kiḷaiyava ruvakaiyiṟ keḻuma īṉṟaṉaḷ
       vaḷaiyava ḷicōmati maintaṉ taṉṉaiyē.         78
       --------
       (pāṭam)*ceṉṟanāḷ.

   urai: icōtaraṉ - yacōtaraṉākiya iḷavaracaṉ, iḷaiyavaḷ -
   iḷamaiyuṭaiyavaḷāṉa amiḻtamatiyiṉ, eḻil - aḻakaiyum, nalam -
   kuṇañceykaikaḷiṉ nalattaiyum, ēntu koṅkaiyiṉ viḷaipayaṉ - uyarnta
   mulaiyiṭattē peṟum iṉpattaiyum, viḻaintu - virumpi, cellum nāḷ -
   iṉitirukkuṅkālattil, kiḷaiyavar - cuṟṟattār, uvakaiyiṉ keḻuma -
   makiḻcciyāl āravārikka, vaḷaiyavaḷ - vaḷaiyaṇinta avvamirtamati,
   icōmatimaintaṉ taṉṉai - yacōmatiyeṉṉum makaṉai, īṉṟaṉaḷ - peṟṟāḷ, e -
   ṟu.
   yacōtaraṉ amiḻtamatiyiṉ iḷamai, eḻil, ēntu koṅkai eṉṟa ivaṟṟiṉ nalattai
   viḻaintu iṉpuṟṟoḻukiyatum, avvoḻukkattiṉ payaṉāka, yacōmatiyaip
   peṟṟatum itaṉkaṭ kūṟiṉār. kāmaviṉpattukkuc ciṟapputtaruvatu
   iḷamaiyātaliṉ, "iḷaiyavaḷ" eṉṟeṭuttu, amiḻtamatiyiṉ piṟa eḻil
   nalaṅkaḷaiyum koṅkaippayaṉaiyum vitantōtiṉār. yacōmatiyiṉ piṟappu
   acōkaṉatu vaḻiyeñcāmaikku ākkamātaliṉ, "kiḷaiyavar uvakaiyiṟ keḻuma
   vīṉṟaṉaḷ" eṉṟār. ivvāṟu maṉaikku viḷakkākiya amiḻtamati taṉakku
   viḷakkan tarum makaṉaip peṟṟāḷeṉṟaṟku, "vaḷaiyavaḷ icōmatimaintaṉtaṉṉai
   yīṉṟaṉaḷ" eṉa avaḷmēlēṟṟiṉār. avaḷuṭaiya eḻilnalac ciṟappai,
   yacōtaraṉmēlum makaṟpēṟṟiṉai avaḷmēlum kūṟiyatu, piṉṉar avaḷ taṉ
   nalattaip poruntā voḻukkattil īṭupaṭuttik keṭuvatum, yacōmati
   ciṟappuṟṟu mēmpaṭuvatum kuṟikkoṇṭu pōlum. vaḷaiyavaḷ, cuṭṭu.
   -----------
   acōkaṉ taṉakku mutumai varavu kāṇṭal
   maṟṟornāḷ maṉṉavaṉ makiḻntu kaṇṇaṭi
       paṟṟuvā ṉaṭitoḻap* paṭiva nōkkuvāṉ
       oṟṟaivār kuḻaṉmayi rucci veṇmaiyai
       uṟṟuṟā vakaiyatai yuḷaintu kaṇṭaṉaṉ.         79

   urai:- orunāḷ - ivvāṟu celluṅkālattu oru nāḷ, maṉṉavaṉ - vēntaṉākiya
   acōkaṉ, kaṇṇaṭi paṟṟuvāṉ - kaṇṇāṭi yēntikkāṭṭuvōṉ, aṭitoḻa - aṭipaṇintu
   ataṉaik kāṭṭa, paṭivam makiḻntu nōkkuvāṉ - taṉ vaṭivattaik kāṇavirumpi
   atiṟ kāṇpavaṉ- vārkuḻal oṟṟaimayir - nīṇṭa kaṭaikuḻaṉṟa mayiroṉṟu, ucci
   - nuṉiyil, veṇmaiyai uṟṟu - veḷḷai niṟattaip porunti, uṟāvakaiyatai -
   puṟattē naṉku tōṉṟāvakai maṟaintiruppatai, uḷaintukaṇṭaṉaṉ - maṉam
   varuntip pārttāṉ e-ṟu.
   paṇṭaiyaracarpāl aṭaippai tāṅkuvōr, aṭiyīṭēntuvōr,kaṇṇaṭiyēntuvōr eṉak
   kuṟṟavēl ceyvār palar iruntaimaiyiṉ īṇṭuk kaṇṇaṭiyēntuvōṉaik
   kuṟippittār. taṉṉai naṉku oppaṉai ceytukoḷḷak karutik kaṇṇāṭi
   nōkkuṅkāl; makiḻcci piṟattal iyalpātalpaṟṟi, "makiḻntu nōkkuvāṉ" eṉṟār.
   avaṉ makiḻcci muṟṟum varuttamāy māṟiṟṟeṉpatu, "uḷaintu kaṇṭaṉaṉ"
   eṉpataṉāl peṟṟām. oru mayir nuṉiyil naraittut taṉ naraipput tōṉṟāvakai
   maṟaintiruppiṉum ataṉaik kaṇṭu kaḷaintavaṉ, maṉattē varuttamuṟṟāṉeṉa
   vaṟika. ataṟkuriya kāraṇattai mēlvarum pāṭṭukkaḷāl uraikkiṉṟār.
   -----------
   acōkaṉ taṉ maṉattē palapaṭa niṉaittal
   vaṇṭaḷir puraitiru mēṉi mātarār
       kaṇṭaka luṟavaru kaḻiya mūppitu
       uṇṭeṉi luḷaintika luruva villitaṉ
       vaṇṭuḷa kaṇaipayaṉ maṉitark keṉṟaṉaṉ.         80
       ---------
       (pāṭam)* paṟṟivāṉaṭitoḻa.

   urai:- vaṇtaḷirpurai - vaḷaviya māntaḷirpōlum, tirumēṉi mātarār -
   aḻakiyamēṉiyuṭaiyamakaḷir, kaṇṭu - pārttavuṭaṉ, akaluṟa - aruvaruttu
   nīṅkumpaṭiyāka, varu - varukiṉṟa, kaḻiya mūppu - āṇṭu mikutiyaiyuṭaiya
   mutumaipparuvamāṉatu, uṇṭueṉil - uḷatāyiṉ atu varaiyiṟṟāṉ, uḷaintu-
   maṉam tirintu, ikal - māṟu pāṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya, uruva villitaṉ - aḻakiya
   villiṉaiyuṭaiya maṉmataṉatu, vaṇṭuuḷa kaṇai - vaṇṭu moykkum pūvākiya
   ampu, maṉitarkkup payaṉ - makkaṭkuk kāma vēṭkaiyākiya payaṉai
   viḷaivikkum, eṉṟaṉaṉ - eṉṟu taṉakkuḷḷē collikkoṇṭāṉ. e-ṟu.
   taḷiraṉap potuppaṭakkūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, ciṟappuṭaiya mān taḷir koḷḷappaṭṭatu,
   "māviṉ avirtaḷirpōlum mēṉiyar"1 eṉṟu cāṉṟōrum kūṟutal kāṇka. kaṇṭār
   maṉam kavarum aḻaku tikaḻum mēṉiyait "tirumēṉi" yeṉṟār. mikka
   kātalarāyiṉum, mūppeytiyavaḻi akkātalāṟ peṟum kāmaviṉpamkuṉṟutaliṉ,
   avar kāmak kātalum nīṅkum eṉpār, "kaṇṭu akaluṟa" eṉṟum, yāṇṭu mikamika
   mūppumikutaliṉ, "kaḻiya mūppu" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. eṉil - eṉṟāl.
   kāmavēṭkaiyuṟṟārkku maṉattuṉpam iyalpātaliṉ "uḷaintu" eṉṟār. kāmaṉ
   uruviliyāyiṉum, avaṉ vil uruvuṭaittāy, cāṉṟōrkku accam taruvatātaliṉ,
   "uruvavilli" eṉṟār.vaṇṭuḷa kaṇai, vaṇṭu taṅkiyirukkum pūṅkaṇai.
   malarkkaṇai, kāma vēṭkaiyaik kiḷarac ceyvatu, payaṉātaliṉ, ataṉai,
   "vaṇṭuḷakaṇai payaṉ" eṉṟāṉ.

       1. muruku. 143-4.

   acōkaṉ iḷamaiyiṉ vaḷamaiyiṉmaiyait taṉṉuḷ niṉaittal
   iḷamaiyi ṉiyalpitu vāku meṉṉiṉiv*
       vaḷamaiyi liḷamaiyai maṉattu vaippateṉ
       kiḷaimaiyu maṉaiyatē keḻumu nammuḷat
       taḷaimaiyai viṭuvatē takuva tāmiṉi.         81
       ------
       (pāṭam)* vāyaveṉṉiṉi.

   urai:-iḷamaiyiṉ iyalpu ituvākum eṉṉiṉ - iḷamaipparuvattiṉ iyalpu
   ittaṉmaittākumāyiṉ. ivvaḷamaiyil iḷamaiyai - ivvaḷappamillāta iḷamaip
   paruvattai, maṉattuvaippatu eṉ - makkaḷ tam maṉattē poruḷāka
   matittukkoḷvatu eṟṟukku, kiḷaimaiyum aṉṉatē - ēṉaiyōr uṟavumuṟaimaiyum
   attaṉmaittēyākum, iṉi - iṉimēl, nam uḷam keḻumum taḷaimaiyai -
   nammaṉattē eḻum aṉpiṉai, viṭuvatu takuvatām - paṟṟaṟa viṭṭoḻippatē
   cālpākum e-ṟu.
   iḷamaikuṉṟātu iṉpattiṟ cemmān tirukkiṉṟēmeṉat tarukkiya vuṇarvāl
   makiḻcciyuṟṟuk kaṇṇāṭi nōkkiya vēntaṟku mayiriṉ narai tōṉṟi
   avviḷamaiyiṉ kaḻiviṉaik kāṭṭakkaṇṭu, ataṉ nilaiyāmaiyiṉaiyum tērntu
   kūṟukiṉṟātaliṉ, "iḷamai yiṉiyalpituvākum eṉṉiṉ" eṉṟāṉ. iḷamaiyē vaḷamai
   yeṉṟum, "iḷamaiṟ ciṟantavaḷamaiyumillai”1 eṉṟum kūṟappaṭuvaṉa koṇṭu,
   ataṉaip pēṇutalum ulakiyalātaliṉ, ataṉai muṉintu, "ivvaḷamaiyil
   iḷamaiyai maṉattu vaippatu eṉ" eṉṟāṉ; eṉavē, iḷamaiyiṉ vaḷamaiyāvatu,
   nilai pēṟuṭaittāy iṉpanukarccittāy iruttaleṉpatum, atukaḻinta vaḻic
   celvamum iṉpantārā teṉpatum peṟṟām" iḷamai kaḻinta piṉṟai vaḷamai,
   kāmam tarutalumiṉṟē2" eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. maṉaivi makkaḷ
   aṭiyākap piṟakkum kiḷaiñar uṟavum, aracu, celvam mutaliya paṟṟukkōṭāka,
   "uṟiṉ naṭṭu aṟiṉ" oruvum iyalpiṟṟātalai yuṇarntu niṟṟaliṉ, "kiḷaimaiyum
   aṇaiyatē" eṉṟāṉ. aṉpiṉpayaṉ ataṉaicceyvōrum ceyyappaṭuvōrum
   piṇippuṇṭuniṟṟalātaliṉ, ataṉait "taḷaimai" yeṉṟum, atukāraṇamāka
   vēṇṭutal vēṇṭāmai tōṉṟit tuṉpam viḷaittaliṉ, "viṭuvatu takuvatām" eṉṟum
   kūṟiṉāṉ. ēkāram iraṇṭum tēṟṟam.

       1,2 naṟ. 126.

   puṇṇiyamē ceyaṟpāṟṟeṉa acōkaṉ niṉaintu teḷital
   muntucey nalviṉai muḷaippa vittalaic
       cintaicey poruḷoṭu celva meytiṉām
       muntaiyiṉ mummaṭi muyaliṟ* puṇṇiyam
       intira vulakamum meyta lāmeṉā+         82
       --------
       (pāṭam) *muyaṉṟu. + meytaṟpāṟṟeṉā; meytaṟpālatē

   urai:- muntu cey nalviṉai - muṉṉaip piṟaviyiṟ ceyta nalviṉaiyāṉatu, it
   talai muḷaippa - ippiṟaviyil taṉ payaṉait tōṟṟuvikka, cintaicey
   poruḷoṭu - uyarntōr matikkattakka nallaṟivuṭaṉ;celvam eytiṉām -araca
   pōkattaiyum aṭaintaḷḷōm, muntaiyiṉ- muṉṉaiyiṉum, mummaṭi puṇṇiyam
   muyaliṉ - mūṉṟu maṭaṅku mikutiyāka nalviṉaiyaic ceyyiṉ, intira
   vulakamum eytalām-tēvarulakattup peṟum pōkattaiyum peṟalām, eṉā-eṉṟu
   e-ṟu.
   oruvaṉ ceyyum viṉai avaṉ uyiriṉkaṭkiṭantu, avaṉ pukkuḻippakum
   iyalpiṟṟeṉpavātaliṉ, "muntuceynalviṉai muḷaippa vittalai" eṉṟār.
   "muḷaippa" eṉṟa kuṟippu, nal viṉaiyākiyavitai uyirākiyanilattu
   vitaikkappeṟutalai yuṇartti niṉṟatu; "vevviṉai ceyyumāntar uyireṉum
   nilattuvitti avviṉaiviḷaiyuḷuṇṇum avviṭattu"1eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal
   kāṇka. oruvaṉ cintaiceyvaṉa, immaikkaṇ pukaḻum maṟumaik kaṇ paṇṇiyamum
   vīṭupēṟumātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṟkētuvāya pukaḻum uṟutipporuḷaṟivum kūṟuvār,
   "cintai cey poruḷoṭu celvam eytiṉām" eṉṟār.celvattiṉum poruḷciṟantu
   niṟṟaliṉ, "poruḷoṭu" eṉṟār.piṟanta piṟappāl, iṟanta piṟaviyiṟ ceyta
   viṉaiyiṉait terintu koṇṭāṉātaliṉ, "cintaicey poruḷoṭu celvameytiṉā"
   meṉṟum, "muntu ceynalviṉai" yeṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉeṉavuṇarka. mīṭṭum muṉ ceyta
   aḷavātal, irumaṭiyātal nalviṉai ceyyiṉ, maṟumaiyil maṇṇulakattē
   aracarpōkamum, avar ellāruḷḷum talaiyāya pōkamumē eytumeṉat teḷitaliṉ,
   "mummaṭi puṇṇiyam muyaliṉ" eṉṟum, ataṉāṟpayaṉ viṇṇulattuppeṟum
   intirapōkam eytumeṉṟukaṇṭu, "intiravulakamum eytalām" eṉṟum taṉakkuḷ
   terinturaippāṉāyiṉaṉ. ivvārāycci yuṭaiyavaṉ, nilaiyilpōkamākiya
   intiravulakattaiyum piṉṉart tuṟantu vīṭupēṟṟiṟkē muyalvāṉ eṉṟaṟika.

       1.cīvaka.2762.

   acōkaṉ tuṟavu
   iṉaiyaṉa niṉaivuṟīi yacōta raṉeṉun
       taṉayaṉai nilamakaḷ talaiva ṉākeṉak
       kaṉamaṇi vaṉaimuṭi kavittuk kāvalaṉ
       puṉaivaḷai matimati pulampap pōyiṉāṉ.         83

   urai:- kāvalaṉ-vēntaṉāṉa acōkaṉ, iṉaiyaṉa niṉivuṟīi-ittaṉmaiyāṉavaṟṟai
   niṉaintu, yacōtaraṉ eṉum taṉayaṉai-yacōtaraṉ eṉappaṭum taṉ makaṉai,
   nila makaḷ talaivaṉ āka-nilattukkaracaṉākuka, eṉa-eṉṟu colli, kaṉamaṇi
   vaṉaimuṭi kavittu-periya maṇikaḷaik koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa muṭiyaic cūṭi,
   puṉaivaḷai matimati pulampa-vaḷaiyaṇinta cantiramatiyākiya taṉtēvi
   taṉittu varunta, pōyiṉāṉ-araciyal toṭarpu tuṟantu ceṉṟāṉ e-ṟu.
   "muntaiyiṉ mummuṭi muyaliṟ puṇṇiyam, intiravulakamum eytalām" (82)
   eṉpataṉōṭu, immai maṟumai vīṭupēṟukaṭku ētuvākum iyalpuṭaiya
   viṉaittiṟamum, avaṟṟiṉ kaṭṭum, ak kaṭṭiṉ nīṅkun tiṟamum, nīṅkāvaḻip
   paṭum tuṉpamum eṉṟa ivaṟṟaiyum palvēṟu nilaiyāmaikaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum
   niṉait tamai tōṉṟa, "iṉaiyaṉa niṉaivuṟīi" eṉṟār. nāṭṭuvēntaṉai,
   nāṭākiya makaṭkuk kaṇavaṉeṉpavākaliṉ, vēntaṉāvāṉai, "nila
   makaṭṭalaivaṉāka" eṉṟāṉ. colliyataṉōṭu amaiyātu, man tiriccuṟṟattārōṭu
   kalantu tirumuṭiyum yacōtararkkuccūṭṭiṉāṉ eṉṟār. piṉpu acōkaṉ,
   araciyaṟṟoṭarpu nīṅki, maṉaiviyākiya cantiramatiyaiyum tuṟakka
   veṇṇiyapōtu, avaḷ pirivāṟṟāmai kūṟi varuntavum avaṭku iṇaṅkātu
   tuṟavēpūṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ eṉpār "puṉaivaḷaimatimati pulampap pōyiṉāṉ" eṉṟār.
   vaḷai puṉaimatimatiyeṉpatu etukainōkki, "puṉaivaḷaimatimati" yeṉa
   vantatu, "puṉaivaḷai" eṉa vaḷaiyai vitantōtiyatu, acōkaṉatu tuṟavumoḻi
   ceviyiṟ paṭṭamāttiraiyē avaḷ muṉkaiyiṉ nillātu kaḻaṉṟōṭi avaḷatu
   pirivāṟṟāmaiyai meyppaṭuttik kāṭṭiṟṟātaliṉ. iḵtu uṭampu naṉi curuṅkal.
   matimati-cantiramati.

   acōkaṉ kuṇatara muṉikaḷpāl aṟaṅkēṭṭut tavam ceyvāṉ vaṉattukkuc cellutal
   kuraikaḻa lacōkaṉmeyk kuṇata raṟpaṇin
       taraicarka ḷaimpatiṟ ṟiruvar tammuṭaṉ
       uraiceya aruntavat* tuṟavu koṇṭupōy
       varaiyuṭai vaṉamatu maruvi ṉāṉarō.         84
       ---------------
       (pāṭam)*laruntavat.

   urai:- kuraikaḻal acōkaṉ-olikkiṉṟa vīrakaṇṭai yaṇinta vēntaṉāṉa acōkaṉ,
   meykkuṇataraṉ paṇintu- meyyaṟiviṉaiyuṭaiya kuṇatara muṉikaḷai vaṇaṅki
   avar uraitta aṟaṅkaḷaik kēṭṭu, araicarkaḷ aimpatiṟṟiruvar tammuṭaṉ
   -tuṇaiyāy vanta aracar nūṟṟuvaruṭaṉ, urai ceya
   aruntavattu-collutaṟkariya tavakkōlam pūṇṭu, varaiyuṭai
   vaṉamatu-malaiyaiyuṭaiya kāṭoṉṟai, pōy maruviṉāṉ-pōyc cērntāṉ e-ṟu.
   meyyaṟivupeṟṟut tavavoḻukkam ciṟantuniṟṟaliṉ, kuṇatara muṉikaḷai
   "meykkuṇataraṉ" eṉṟār. ivarpāl acōkaṉ caiṉa tarumamum, caiṉatīkkaiyum
   peṟṟāṉ eṉṟaṟkup "paṇintu" eṉṟār; tīkkaiyum aṟamum peṟumuṉpum piṉpum
   paṇitalviṉai nikaḻtaliṉ "paṇintu" eṉa uraikkappaṭṭaṉa; ittarumamum
   tīkkaiyum ācārāṅkameṉṉum nūluṭkūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; avai
   pañcaparamēṭṭikaḷiṉ patamupatēcittal, mayirpaṟittal mutalāyiṉa.
   pēraracaṉātaliṉ, ciṟṟaracar nūṟṟuvar uṭaṉceṉṟāreṉavaṟika. tavatturuvu,
   tavavēṭam; "uṟṟanōynōṉṟal uyirkkuṟu kaṇceyyāmai, aṟṟē tavattukkuru"1
   eṉṟapaṭi, nōṉpaiyum uṟukaṇceyyāmaiyaiyum koḷḷalumoṉṟu. varaiyuṭai
   vaṉam, malaiyaiccārnta kāṭu; mūṅkilkaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṭumām.

       1. kuṟaḷ. 261.

   yacōtaraṉ nāṭāṭci mēṟkoḷḷal
   erimaṇi imaikkum pūṇā ṉicōtara ṉiruni lattāṭ*
       korumaṇit tilakam pōlu muñcayi ṉikku nātaṉ†
       arumaṇi muṭikoḷ ceṉṉi yaracaṭip paṭuttu yarnta
       kurumaṇik kuṭaiyiṉ nīḻaṟ kuvalayaṅ kāval koṇṭāṉ.         85
       ---------
       (pāṭam)* ṉirunilattārkku, ṉirunilattukku, †vēntaṉ

   urai:- erimaṇi imaikkum pūṇāṉ-viḷaṅkukiṉṟa maṇikaḷālākiya oḷivīcum
   aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyuṭaiyaṉākiya, icōtaraṉ-yacōtaraṉ, irunilattāṭku-periya
   nilamākiya maṭantaikku, oru maṇittilakam pōlum-oppaṟṟa aḻakiya tilakam
   pōlvatākiya, uñcayiṉikku nātaṉ- uccayiṉi nakarattukku aracaṉāy, arumaṇi
   muṭikoḷ ceṉṉi aracu-ariya maṇikaḷāl iyaṉṟa muṭiyaṇinta
   talaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya aracarkaḷai, aṭippaṭuttu-taṉṉaṭi vaṇaṅkac ceytu,
   uyarnta-ōṅkiya, kurumaṇik kuṭaiyiṉ nīḻal-niṟamuṭaiya māṇikkaṅkaḷ
   vaittiḻaitta koṟṟak kuṭaiyiṉ nīḻalilē, kuvalayam-ulakam taṅka, kāval
   koṇṭāṉ-āṭcipuriyattalaippaṭṭāṉ e-ṟu.
   acōkaṉ tuṟavupūṇa niṉaintavuṭaṉē yacōtaraṉai muṭi cūṭṭi
   aracaṉākkiviṭṭāṉātalāl, "uñcayiṉikkunātaṉ" eṉṟār. irunilattāḷ,
   nilamakaḷ.periyanakaraṅkaḷai nilamakaṭku" tilakamākak kūṟuvatu
   kavimarapu; "nilamakaḷ mukamō tilakamō kaṇṇō"1 eṉṟu kamparum kūṟutal
   kāṇka. nāṭukākkum vēntar ēṉaiyaracarait taṉ aṭippaṭutti uyartalē taṉ
   araciyaṟkuc ciṟap peṉṟum, valikku māṇpeṉṟum karutupavātaliṉ, ataṉaic
   ceytāṉ yacōtaraṉ eṉṟārāyiṟṟu. koṟṟakkuṭai veḷḷi tāyiṉum, māṇikkaṅkaḷum
   iḻaikkap peṟutalpaṟṟi, "kurumaṇikkuṭai" eṉṟār. kuvalayam, nilamākiya
   vaṭṭam. pūṇāṉākiya icōtaraṉ nātaṉāyk kāvalpūṇṭāṉ eṉka. kuvalayaṅkāval,
   vikāram.

       1.kampa.pāla.

   yacōtaraṉ celvac cerukkāl aṟattiṟam nekiḻttal
   tiruttaku kumaraṉ celvac cerukkiṉāl nerukkup paṭṭē
       marutteṟi kaṭaliṟ poṅki maṟukiya maṉatta ṉāki
       urutteḻu ciṉattiṟ ceṉṟa vuḷḷameym moḻiyō ṭoṉṟi
       arutticey tarutta kāmat taṟattiṟa maṟattu ṟantāṉ.         86

   urai:- tiruttaku kumaraṉ-tirumakaḷiṉ viruppam takkirukkiṉṟa
   aracakumaraṉāṉa yacōtaraṉ, celvac cerukkiṉāl-celvamikutiyāl uḷatākum
   mayakkattāl, nerukkup paṭṭu-aṭarppuṇṭu, maruttu eṟi kaṭaliṉ-kāṟṟāl
   mōtappaṭṭa kaṭal poṅki alamaruvatupōla, poṅki maṟukiya
   maṉattaṉāki-avāmutaliyavaṟṟāl poṅkik kalaṅkiya maṉamuṭaiyaṉāy, uruttu
   eḻu ciṉattiṉ-mikku eḻukiṉṟa ciṉamutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ, ceṉṟa-paṭarkiṉṟa,
   uḷḷam mey moḻiyōṭu oṉṟi-maṉam, uṭal, col eṉṟa mūṉṟum oruppaṭṭu niṟka,
   arutta kāmattu arutti ceytu-poruḷiṉpaṅkaḷilē vēṭkai mikak koṇṭu,
   aṟattiṟam aṟat tuṟantāṉ-aṟattiṉ vakaikaḷai muṟṟum kaiviṭṭoḻukuvāṉāyiṉāṉ
   e-ṟu
   puṇṇiyamuḷḷaḷavum oruvaṉaippaṟṟi niṉṟu, atu nīṅkiya vaḻi nīṅku
   miyalpuṭaiya tirumakaḷ, puṇṇiyamuṭaimaiyāl araca pōkantuykkum
   yacōtaraṉpāl nīṅkātirukkiṉṟamai tōṉṟa "tiruttaku kumaraṉ;" eṉṟum, tāṉ
   peṟṟa celvattiṟkut tāṉ mēṟpaṭṭa talaivaṉātalai yuṇarātu, ataṟku
   aṭimaiyāyt taṉ iyaṟkaiyaṟivai yiḻantāṉeṉṉaṟkuc "cerukkiṉāl
   nerukkuppaṭṭu" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. "puṇṇiyamulantapiṉ poruḷilārkaḷaik,
   kaṇṇilar tuṟantiṭuṅ kaṇikaimārkaḷpōl eṇṇilaḷ ikaḻntiṭum"1 eṉṟum;
   "paṟṟiṉar pālaḷ"2 eṉṟum cāṉṟōr kūṟutal kāṇka. taṉṉaiyuṭaiyāṉ
   eḷiyaṉāyiṉ, avaṉai mayakkuvatu celvattiṉ ciṟappiyalpātaliṉ, "celvac
   cerukkiṉāl nerukkuppaṭṭā" ṉeṉṟumām; "maṉṉal ciṟitāy mayakkam peritākip
   puṉmaiyuṟukkum puraiyil arumporuḷ" eṉṟu vaḷaiyāpatiyum kūṟiṟṟu.
   "marutteṟikaṭal" eṉṟa uvamaiyāl avā mutaliyavaṟṟāl yacōtaraṉ
   alaikkappaṭṭamai peṟutum. ciṉattai vitantōtiyataṉāl, ataṟkētuvāya
   kāmamum, payaṉākiya mayakkamum koḷka. kāmavekuḷi mayak kaṅkaḷē ataṟku
   neṟiyāyiṉamaiyiṉ, anneṟikkaṇ avaṉuṭaiya niṉaivum ceyalum collum
   oruppaṭṭu nilaviṉa eṉpār "ciṉat tiṟceṉṟa uḷḷameymmoḻiyōṭoṉṟi" eṉṟār. iv
   viyalpuṭaiyārkku vēṇṭappaṭuvaṉa kāmaviṉpamum, ataṉai iṉitu nukarat
   tuṇaiceyyum poruḷumē yātaliṉ, avaṟṟai, "arutti ceytu" , "aṟattiṟam
   aṟattuṟantāṉ" eṉṟār. arutti, ārvam,
   -------------
       1 cūḷā. mutti. 15. 2 cūḷā.mutti 21.

   poruḷiṉpaṅkaḷai arutticeytoḻukum tiṟam kūṟal
   añcuta lilātu tevva raviyamē laṭarttuc ceṉṟu
       vañcaṉai palavu nāṭi vakuppaṉa vakuttu maṉṉaṉ
       puñciya poruḷu nāṭum puṇartiṟam puṇarntu neñcil
       tuñcuta lilāta kaṇṇaṉ tuṇivaṉa tuṇintu niṉṟāṉ.         87

   urai:- tevvar aviya-taṉakku añcip paṇintoḻukāta pakaivēntar vali
   keṭumāṟu, añcutal ilātu-accam ciṟitumiṉṟi, mēlceṉṟu-paṭaiyuṭaṉ
   mikkucceṉṟu porutu, vañcaṉai palavum nāṭi-avar ceyyum vañcaṉai palavum
   ārāyntaṟintu, vakuppaṉa vakuttu-avarai vēṟaṟ poruṭṭut tāṉum vañcaṉaikaḷ
   palavaṟṟaiyum nāṭic ceytu, puñciya poruḷum-avaruṭaiya mikkatākiya
   poruḷum, nāṭum-ataṟku varuvāyākiya nāṭum, puṇar tiṟam-tāṉ kavarntu
   kōṭaṟkuriya cūḻccittiṟaṅkaḷai, neñcil puṇarntu-maṉattē ārāyntu koṇṭu,
   tuñcutal ilāta kaṇṇaṉ-nāḷum kaṇṇuṟakkam illāṉāy, tuṇivaṉa
   tuṇintu-ceyyattakuvaṉavaṟṟaic ceytu, niṉṟāṉ-attuṟaiyilē teḷiviṉṟi
   yoḻukuvā ṉāyiṉāṉ e-ṟu.
   pakai vēntarai veṉṟu oṭukki nāṭṭil nalakkuṟaivu nikaḻā vakaiyiṟ kāttal
   vēntarkku iyalpāyiṉum, acceyaṟkaṇ aṟamallatu nikaḻvatu eṇṇi yañcuvatu
   nalvēntar maṉappaṇpāka, ivaṉ, ataṟku añciṉāṉillai yeṉṟaṟku,
   "añcutalilātu" eṉṟum, appakaiyaracar vañcaṉai ceytārāyiṉum, aḵtu
   aṟamaṉṟeṉa voḻiyātu tāṉum avaṟṟaic ceytamai tōṉṟa, "vakuppaṉa vakuttu"
   eṉṟum, avarkkuriya poruḷum nāṭum kavarum tiṟamē niṉaintu, taṉ poruḷum
   nāṭum mikutaṟkēṟpaṉavaṟṟai niṉaintila ṉeṉṟaṟku, "puṇartiṟam neñciṟ
   puṇarntu" eṉṟum, niṉaivoṭu nillātu ceyaṟkaṇṇum ceytoḻukiṉā ṉeṉpār
   "tuṇivaṉatuṇintu niṉṟāṉ" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. itaṉāl, "aṟattiṟam
   aṟattuṟantāṉ" poruḷ ceyal kūṟappaṭṭatu.
   -----------
   tōṭalār kōtai mātar tuyariyiṟ ṟoṭutta viṉpap
       pāṭalo ṭiyainta paṇṇiṉ icaiccuvai parukip palkāl
       ūṭalaṅ kiṉiya miṉṉiṉ olkiya makaḷi rāṭum
       nāṭakam nayantu kaṇṭum nāḷcila cellac ceṉṟāṉ.         88

   urai:-tōṭu alar kōtaimātar-itaḻkaḷaiyuṭaiya pūkkaḷāl toṭukkappaṭṭa
   mālaiyaṇinta kūntalaiyuṭaiya pāṭalmakaḷir,
   tuyariyiṉtoṭutta-yāḻnarampiṉāl toṭuttup pāṭum, iṉpappāṭaloṭu iyainta
   paṇṇiṉ-iṉpattataittarum pāṭaloṭu iyaintu niṟkum paṇṇiṭattu eḻum,
   icaiccuvai paruki-icaiyamutākiya amutattaip paruki, palkāl-paṉmuṟaiyum,
   ūṭal aṅku iṉiya-ūṭiya viṭattum puṇarntāṟpōlum iṉpattait tarukiṉṟa,
   miṉṉil olkiya-miṉṉalaippōla acaintāṭum, makaḷir- āṭalmakaḷir, āṭum
   nāṭakam nayantu kaṇṭum-āṭukiṉṟa nāṭakaṅkaḷai virumpippārttu makiḻntum,
   nāḷcila cella- nāṭkaḷ cila kaḻiya, ceṉṟāṉ-oḻukivaralāṉāṉ e-ṟu.
   itaṉāl iṉpattai arutticeytu oḻukum tiṟam kūṟukiṉṟār. tōṭu-pūvitaḻ;
   tuyari-yāḻnarampu; "tūmam kamaḻum kōtai toṭuttatuyarimulaiyāt,
   tēmeṉkītam pālāc curantu"1 eṉap piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. "paṇṇeṉṉām pāṭaṟ
   kiyai piṉṟēl"2 eṉpavākaliṉ "pāṭalo ṭiyainta paṇṇiṉ" eṉṟār.
   kāmakkātaluṭaiya makaḷir ūṭutalum kāmattiṟ kiṉpamātaliṉ
   "ūṭalaṅkiṉiyamakaḷir" eṉṟār. "ūṭiṉum puṇarnta tottiṉiyavaḷ"3 eṉṟu
   piṟarum kūṟuvar. paruki yeṉpuḻi eṇṇummai vikārattāl tokkatu.
   ---------------
       1 cīvaka. 921. 2.kuṟaḷ. 573. 3.cīvaka. 2004

   yacōtaraṉ amirtamatiyoṭu kūṭi yiṉpuṟum ciṟappu
   maṟṟornāḷ maṉṉar tammai maṉaipuka viṭuttu mālaik
       koṟṟavē lavaṉṟaṉ kōyiṟ kuḷirmaṇik kūṭa moṉṟiṟ
       cuṟṟuvār tiraiyiṟ ṟūmaṅ kamaḻtuyiṟ cēkkai tuṉṉik
       kaṟṟaivār kavari vīcak kaḷiciṟan tiṉitu runtāṉ.         89

   urai:- maṟṟu ōr nāḷ-vēṟorunāḷ, maṉṉar tammai-aracavaikkaṇ vantirunta
   aracarkaḷai, maṉaipuka viṭuttu-tattam maṉaikkuc cellaviṭuttu,
   mālaikoṟṟam- mālaiyum veṟṟiyumuṭaiya, vēlavaṉ-vēlēntiya vēntaṉākiya
   yacōtaraṉ, taṉ kōyil-taṉ antappurattēyuḷḷa, kuḷirmaṇik kūṭam
   oṉṟil-kuḷirnta oḷivīcum maṇikaḷāl iyaṉṟa oru kūṭattil
   amaikkappaṭṭirunta, cuṟṟuvār tiraiyiṉ-cuṟṟilum nīṇṭa tiraikaḷaiyuṭaiya,
   tūmam kamaḻ-naṟumpukaiyiṉ maṇamkamaḻum, tuyilcēkkai
   tuṉṉi-paḷḷiyaṇaiyaiyaṭaintu, kaṟṟaivār kavari vīca- kaṟṟaiyākavuḷḷa
   cāmaraiyai makaḷir vīca, kaḷiciṟantu- kāmakkaḷippu mikuntu, iṉitu
   iruntāṉ-amirtamatiyiṉ varavunōkki iṉitiruntāṉ e-ṟu.
   "maṉṉartammai maṉaipuka viṭuttu" eṉavē, iravuppōtiṉ varavu kuṟittavāṟu
   peṟṟām.pakaṟpōtil makaḷir toṭutta pāṭṭum, āṭiya nāṭakamum kaṇṭu kāmam
   ciṟantu antappurattai yaṭaikiṉṟāṉātaliṉ, nērē paḷḷiyaṟaiyait tuṉṉiṉāṉ
   eṉṟār. "kuḷirmaṇikkūṭam" eṉṟār, kāmaveppattāl vetumpivarumāṟu tōṉṟa.
   kaḷi-kāmakkaḷippu. makaḷir kavari vīciyatu, kāmaveppattait taṇittaṟku.
   iṉitiruntāṉ eṉṟār, amirtamatipāl tāṉ nukaravirukkum kāmaviṉpamē
   niṉaintiruttaliṉ.
   ----------
   cilampoṭu cilampit tēṉun tirumaṇi vaṇṭum pāṭak
       kalampala vaṇinta valkuṟ kalaiyoli kalavi yārppa
       nalaṅkalan tiṉiya* kāmar naṟumalart toṭaiya lēpōl
       alaṅkalaṅ kuḻalpiṉ tāḻa amiḻtamuṉ matiya ṇaintāḷ.         90
       ---------
       (pāṭam) *nalaṅkavaṉṟiṉiya.

   urai:- tēṉum-tēṉiṉamum, tirumaṇi vaṇṭum- aḻakiya nīlamaṇipōlum
   vaṇṭiṉamum, pāṭa-pāṭṭicaikka, kalam pala-aṇikalam palavaṟṟōṭu, alkul
   aṇinta kalaiyoli-alkuliṭattē yaṇinta mēkalaiyiṉ oliyāṉatu, cilampoṭu
   kalavi cilampiyārppa-cilampoliyoṭu kalantu mikkolikka, nalam
   kalantu-peṇmainalam mutirntu, iṉiya kāmar naṟumalart toṭaiyalpōl- iṉiya
   aḻakiya maṇaṅkamaḻum pūmālai varutalpōla, alaṅkal-pūmālaicūṭiya, am
   kuḻal piṉtāḻa-aḻakiya kūntalāṉatu mutukiṭattē tāḻntacaiya,
   amiḻtamuṉmati- amutamati, aṇaintāḷ-yacōtaraṉirunta paḷḷiyai yaṭaintāḷ
   e-ṟu.
   tēṉum vaṇṭum pāṭa, kalaiyoli, cilampoṭu kalavi, cilampi yārppa, kuḻal
   piṉ tāḻa, aṇaintāḷ eṉa iyaiyum. iṉik kiṭantapaṭiyē, tēṉum vaṇṭum
   cilampippāṭa, kalaiyoli ēṉaik kalaṉkaḷuṭaṉ kalavi yārppa, kuḻal piṉtāḻa
   aṇaintāḷ eṉiṉumām. tēṉ, vaṇṭiṉattuḷ oṉṟu. "vaṇṭukāḷ makiḻ tēṉiṉaṅkāḷ"1
   eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟutal kāṇka. kalai, mēkalai. naṟumalart toṭaiyal
   aṇaivatupōla aṇaintāḷ eṉka; "maruvaḷarmālaiyor valliyiṉolki yaṉanaṭai
   vāyn, turuvaḷar kāmaṉṟaṉ veṉṟik koṭipōṉ ṟoḷirkiṉṟatē"2 eṉṟu
   tirukkōvaiyāruḷḷum varutal kāṇka.

   āṅkava ḷaṇainta pōḻti ṉaiṅkaṇaik kuricil tanta
       pūṅkaṇai māri veḷḷam porutuvan talaippap pulli
       nīṅkala roruva ruḷpuk kiruvaru moruva rākit
       tēṅkama ḻamaḷi tēmpac ceṟintaṉar tiḷaittu viḷḷār.         91

   urai:- āṅku-avviṭattiṟku, avaḷ aṇaintapōḻtiṉ-avaḷ vantaṭaintapōtu,
   aiṅkaṇaik kuricil- aivakaip pūkkaḷālākiya ampukaḷaiyuṭaiya maṉmataṉ,
   tanta- viṭutta, pūṅkaṇaimāri-malarampu maḻaipōl vantu vīḻa, veḷḷam
   porutu vantu alaippa-kāmaveḷḷamāṉatu aṟivumutaliya karaikaḷai
   alaittukkoṇṭuvantu avartam neñcai varutta, iruvarum pulli- iruvarum
   tammil taḻuvi, nīṅkalar-nīṅkārāy, oruvar uḷ oruvar pukku-oruvar uṭaṟkuḷ
   oruvar pukuntu, oruvarāki-iruvarum oruvarēyāy, tēmkamaḻ
   amaḷitēmpa-tēṉmaṇakkum paṭukkaiyacain tolikka, ceṟintaṉar-puṇarntu,
   tiḷaittu viḷḷār-oruvaroruvaraip piṇitta kai nekiḻātu aṇaimītē
   kiṭantaṉar e-ṟu.
   malarampukaḷai maḻaipōlac corintataṉāl, ataṉ payaṉākiya kāmavēṭkai
   veḷḷamāyp perukutaliṉ "pūṅkaṇai māri veḷḷam" eṉṟār. kāmam kaimmikkavaḻi
   aṟivum niṟaiyum piṟavum nīṅkutaliṉ, avaṟṟaik karaiyākki,
   "porutuvantalaippa" eṉpārāyiṟṟu. oruvaruḷ oruvar pukkup puṇarum
   puṇarcciyai nākar puṇarcciyeṉpar. "nañcuṟṟa kāmam naṉinākariṟṟuytta
   vāṟum"3 eṉṟaviṭattu "nākarōṭuvamai ōruṭampātalum nīṅkalvaṉmaiyumpaṟṟi"
   eṉa varum ataṉ urai kāṇka. viḷḷal, nīṅkutal.
   --------------
       1 cīvaka 897. 2.kōvai 1. 3. cīvaka. 11.

   ---------
   maṭaṅkaṉin tiṉiya nallāḷ vaṉamulaip pōka mellām
       aṭaṅkala ṉayarnta tēṉvā yamirtamum paruki yampoṟ
       paṭaṅkalan* takaṉṟa valkuṟ pāmpaṇai† puṇaiya tāka
       iṭaṅkaḻin toḻivi liṉpak kaṭaliṭai mūḻki ṉāṉē.         92
       --------------
       (pāṭam) *paṭaṅkaṭan. † pāvaiyē.

   urai:- maṭam kaṉintu-iḷamai mikuntu, iṉiya- iṉiyaḷākiya, nallāḷ
   vaṉamulaip pōkamellām-nalla amutamatiyiṉ aḻakiya mulaiyiṭattē peṟum
   iṉpa muḻutum, aṭaṅkalaṉ ayarntu-aṭaṅkāta ārvattōṭu nukarntu, tēṉvāy
   amirtamum paruki-eyiṟṟiṭattūṟiya tēṉpōlum nīraipparuki, ampoṉ pāmpu
   paṭam kalantu aḻakiya pāmpiṉ paṭattaiyottu, akaṉṟa alkulaṇai- akaṉṟa
   alkulākiya paṭukkaiyaiyē, puṇaiyatāka-teppamākakkoṇṭu, iṭam
   kaḻintu-neñciṭam nirampi vaḻintum, oḻivil-nīṅkutalillāta,
   iṉpakkaṭaliṭai-kāmaviṉpak kaṭaṟkuḷḷē, mūḻkiṉāṉ-vīḻntu karaiyēṟa māṭṭātu
   ataṉuḷḷē mūḻkiviṭṭāṉ e-ṟu.
   maṭam, iḷamai; aḻakumām. muṉṉēkūṭi, yacōmati yeṉṉum makaṉaip
   peṟṟuḷaṉāyiṉum, yacōtaraṉ avaḷpāl vēṭkaiyaṭaṅkāṉāyiṉāṉeṉṟaṟku
   "aṭaṅkalaṉayarntu" eṉṟār. kaḻikāmattaṉeṉpatu karuttu. eyiṟūṟiyanīr,
   "pāloṭu tēṉkalantaṟṟu"1 eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟutaliṉ, "tēṉvā yamirtam" eṉṟār.
   parukutalai, atarapāṉam eṉpar. pāmpaṇai eṉpatu pirittukkūṭṭappaṭṭatu;
   alkulākiya pāmpaṇaiyeṉṟu kōṭalu moṉṟu. pāmpiṉ paṭamum puṇaiyum muṟaiyē
   uruvum toḻilum paṟṟi vantaṉa. iṭamkaḻital-neñcakam nirampit tuḷumputal,
   "iṭaṅkaḻikāmamoṭaṭaṅkāṉāki"2 eṉṟu piṟarum kūṟupa.

       1.kuṟaḷ.1121. 2. maṇi. 18:119.

   puṇarcci yavacattāl iruvarum kaṇṇuṟaṅkal
   iṉṉaric cilampun tēṉum eḻilvaḷai niraiyu mārppap
       poṉṉavir tārō ṭāram puṇarmulai porutu poṅka
       maṉṉaṉum maṭantai tāṉum mataṉakō pattiṉ* māṟāyt
       toṉṉalan tolaiya uṇṭār tuyilkoṇṭa viḻika ḷaṉṟē.         93
       ---------
       (pāṭam) *mataṉarākattiṉ † cintaiyiṉ

   urai:- iṉ aric cilampum-iṉiya ōcaiceyyum paralkaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṟcilampum,
   tēṉum -vaṇṭiṉamum, eḻilvaḷainiraiyum-aḻakiya varicaiyuṟṟa
   vaḷaiyalkaḷum, ārppa-olikka, poṉ avir tārōṭu-yacōtaraṉaṇinta
   poṉṉāliyaṉṟa mālaiyum, āram-amutamati yaṇinta muttumālaiyum, puṇarmulai
   porutu poṅka-puṇarcci nikaḻumiṭattu , mulaiyāl tākkuṇṭu mēleḻa,
   maṉṉaṉum maṭantaitāṉum-yacōtaraṉum amutamatiyum, mataṉa kōpattiṉ-kāmak
   kaḷimayakkiṉāl, tolnalam māṟāyt tolaiya-puṇarātamuṉpirunta mēṉinalam
   māṟupaṭṭu meliya,uṇṭār-kāmaviṉpattait tuyttu ayarntārāka viḻikaḷ
   tuyilkoṇṭa- avaruṭaiya kaṇkaḷ uṟaṅkuvaṉavāyiṉa e-ṟu.
   tār, mārpilaṇiyum mālai, iḵtu āṭavarkku urittu, tār eṉap pirittamaiyiṉ,
   āram amirtamati yaṇinta muttumālaikkāyiṟṟu. "maintar taṇṭār makaḷir
   peyyavum;"1 eṉpuḻittār. āṭavarkkurittākak kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
   kāmavēṭkaiyāl neñcu kalaṅkutalpaṟṟi "māṟāy" eṉṟāreṉṟumām. "toṉṉalam
   tolaiyavuṇṭār"eṉṟār mīṭṭum avar ivviṉpam nukarātoḻivatu karuti.
   uṟakkattai avar viṉaiyākkātu, viḻimēl ēṟṟiyataṉāl, uṭal
   ayarntaṉarēyaṉṟi, uḷḷattāl kāmanukarccikkaṇ ayarvuṟṟilareṉṟu koḷka.

       1. pari. 20:20.

   aṭṭapaṅkaṉ icaitta icaiyāl amirtamati aṟivu tirital
   āyiṭai yatti kūṭat tayaleḻun tamirta mūṟac
       cēyiṭaic ceṉṟōr kītañ cevipuka viṭutta lōṭum
       vēyiṭait tōḷi mella viḻittaṉaḷ viyantu nōkkāt
       tīyiṭai meḻuki ṉainta cintaiya† ḷuruki ṉāḷē.         94

   urai:- āyiṭai--appōtu, attikūṭattu ayal eḻuntu-yāṉai kaṭṭumiṭattiṉ
   puṟattē eḻuntu, amirtam ūṟa-kēṭpōr uḷattē iṉpamuṇṭākumāṟu, cēyiṭaic
   ceṉṟa-neṭuntolaivu paṭarntu makkaḷ icaikkiṉṟa, ōr kītam-oru pāṭṭu,
   cevipuka viṭuttalōṭum-cevikkuṭceṉṟu taṉ icaiyiṉpattai yūṭṭavē,
   vēyiṭaittōḷi-mūṅkilpōlum tōḷaiyuṭaiya amirtamati, mella viḻittaṉaḷ-taṉ
   kaṇkaḷai mellat tiṟantu, viyantu nōkkā-avvicaiyiṉpattai viyappōṭu kēṭṭu
   maruṇṭu nāṟṟicaiyum pārttu, tīyiṭai meḻukiṉ-neruppiliṭṭa meḻukupōla,
   nainta cintaiyaḷ-melinta maṉattaiyuṭaiyaḷāy, urukiṉāḷ- urukalāṉāḷ e-ṟu.
   āyiṭai, cuṭṭu nīṇṭu yakaram peṟṟatu. yāṉaikkūṭattiṉ kaṇ irunta
   aṭṭapaṅkaṉ eṭutticaitta pāṭṭu eṅkum parantu, kēṭpōr cevivaḻic ceṉṟu
   iṉpattai yuṇṭupaṇṇiṟṟu eṉpatām, ceṉṟa eṉṉum peyareccat takaram
   vikārattāl tokkatu. iṉṉicai ceṉṟu amirtamatiyiṉ ceviyiṭai yicaittalum
   avaḷ tuyiluṇarntāḷākaliṉ, "mella viḻittaṉaḷ" eṉṟār. viḻittāṭku
   avvicaiyiṉ iṉpam neñciṉai yurukki melivitta teṉṟaṟku,
   "tīyiṭaimeḻukiṉnainta cintaiya ḷurukiṉāḷē" eṉṟār.

   amirtamati matimayaṅkutal
   paṇṇiṉuk koḻuku neñciṟ pāvaiyip paṇkoḷ cevvāy
       aṇṇaluk kamirta māya varivaiyark kuriya pōkam
       viṇṇiṉuk kuḷateṉ ṟeṇṇi veytuyirt tuytal cellāḷ
       maṇṇiṉuk karacaṉ tēvi matimayak kuṟṟi runtāḷ.         95

   urai:- paṇṇiṉukku-pāṭṭiṉpattukku, oḻukum neñciṉ pāvai-nīrāy uruki yōṭum
   neñciṉaiyuṭaiya pāvaipōṉṟa amutamati, ippaṇkoḷ cevvāy
   aṇṇalukku-ivvicaiyamutattaic coriyum civanta vāyaiyuṭaiya
   periyavaṉiṭattē, arivaiyarkku uriya- makaḷir peṟutaṟkurittāyuḷḷa,
   amirtamāya pōkam- pēriṉpattait taravalla kāmapōkam, viṇṇiṉukku
   uḷatu-viṇṇulakattē peṟum pōkamākum, eṉṟu eṇṇi-eṉṟu taṉakkuḷḷē niṉaittu,
   veytuyirttu- perumūcceṟintu, uytal cellāḷ - uyvakaiyaṟiya māṭṭāḷāy,
   maṇṇiṉukku aracaṉ tēvi - avantināṭṭuk karacaṉākiya yacōtaraṟku
   maṉaiviyākiya ivaḷ, matimayakku uṟṟu - aṟivu kalaṅki, iruntāḷ-
   kaiyaṟavupaṭṭiruntāḷ e-ṟu.
   oḻukutal, nīrā yuruki vaḻital; "oḻukunīr āralpārkkum kuruku"1
   eṉpuḻippōla, muṉṉaic ceyyuḷil "urukiṉāḷ" eṉṟārātaliṉ, īṇṭu " oḻukum
   neñciṉ" eṉa vāḷāmoḻintār. nāṇvaḻi yoḻukum pāvaipōla, paṇṇiṉ
   cuvaivaḻiyōṭum neñciṉa ḷātalpaṟṟi, " pāvai" yeṉṟār. itaṉāl,
   maṉavuṇarviṉmaiyum peṟa vaittārāyiṟṟu. ceviccuvai yamutamākiya
   icaiyiṉai vaḻaṅkum ciṟappu nōkki, " cevvāyaṇṇal" eṉa aṭṭapaṅkaḷaik
   karutukiṉṟāḷ. aṇṇalukku, viṇṇiṉukku eṉpuḻi ēḻāvataṉkaṇ nāṉkāvatu vantu
   mayaṅkiṟṟu. amirta māyapōkam, arivaiyarkkuriya pōkam eṉa iyaiyum.
   uḷatu, uḷatākiya pōkam. viṇṇiṉukkuḷateṉṟu eṇṇiya tuṇaiyē,
   peṟutaṟkaritupōlum eṉṟu uṭkoṇṭu varuntaluṟṟāḷ eṉpār, "veytuyirt tuytal
   celvāḷ " eṉṟār. maṇṇiṉukkaracaṉ tēviyāy iṉpakkuṟaipāṭiṉṟiyē
   iruntāḷāyiṉum, aṟivaṟai pōki aṭṭapaṅkaṉpāl pollāk kāmavēṭkai koṇṭu
   maṉam puḻuṅkum puṉmaikaṇṭu ikaḻvār, "maṇṇiṉuk karacaṉ tēvi" eṉṟu
   vitantōtiṉār.
   ---------
   peṇmaiyiṉ puṉmai kūṟal
   miṉṉiṉu nilaiyiṉ ṟuḷḷam viḻaivuṟiṉ viḻainta yāvum*
       tuṉṉiṭu miṉattiṉ tūymai cūḻcciyu moḻiya niṟkum†
       piṉṉuṟu paḻiyiṟ kañcā peṇṇuyirp perumai pēṇā
       teṉṉumim moḻikaṭ kantō vilakkiya māyi ṉāḷē.         96
       -------------
       (pāṭam) * viḻaintavāṟē, † maṉattiṉ.

   urai:-- peṇ - peṇkaṭku, uḷḷam - maṉamāṉatu miṉṉiṉumnilaiyiṉṟu -
   miṉṉalaikkāṭṭilumnilaiyillātatu, viḻaivuṟiṉ - evaṟṟaiyēṉum
   virumpumāyiṉ, viḻainta - avvirumpiyavaṟṟaippaṟṟiya, yāvum tuṉṉiṭum -
   niṉaivu ceyalkaḷaiyē poruntiyirukkum, iṉattiṉ tūymai - tuṇaiyāyiṉār
   kūṟum tūymaiyum. cūḻcciyum - ārāycciyuraiyum, oḻiya-keṭa, niṟkum-tāṉ
   cellakkarutiya neṟiyilēyē ūṉṟi niṟkum, piṉṉuṟu paḻiyiṟku añcā- piṉṉē
   eytukiṉṟa paḻipāvaṅkaṭkuc ciṟitum añcātu, uyirpperumai
   pēṇātu-peṇṇuyirākiya tāṉ piṟanta kuṭipperumaiyum karutātu, eṉṉum
   immoḻikaṭku- eṉṟu aṟintōr aṟinturaitta ivvilakkaṇaṅkaṭku,
   ilakkiyamāyiṉāḷ-ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭāyiṉāḷ, antō-aiyō, ivvamutamatiyiṉ
   iyalpu iruntavāṟu eṉṉē e-ṟu.
   "peṇṇuyir...pērāttiṇmaiyum uṭaiyavalla"1 (47) eṉa muṉpē kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ,
   īṇṭu vāḷā uvamamukattāl, "miṉṉiṉum nilaiyiṉṟuḷḷam" eṉṟum, oru poruṇmēṟ
   cellum viḻaiviṉai nīkkiniṟuttum matukaiyillai yeṉpataiyum muṉṉar
   "peruntiṟalaṟivum uṭaiyavalla" (46) eṉṟataṉai viḷakki, "viḻaivuṟiṉ
   viḻaintayāvum tuṉṉiṭum" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. viḻaivu tōṉṟiyaviṭattu,
   avviḻaiviṉai niṟaivu ceytu kōṭaṟkētuvāya niṉaivuceyalē avar uḷḷattiṟ
   porunti vēṟu evvakaic ceyaṟkaṇṇum cellātoḻitaliṉ, viḻainta yāvum
   tuṉṉiṭum" eṉṟār. viḻainta veṉṉum peyareccam karuviyoṭu muṭintatu.
   tuṇaiyāy oḻukum kuṇavati yeṉpāḷatu tūymaiyum aṟivuraiyum keṭacceyyum
   ivaḷatu ceyal mēlvarum nikaḻccikaḷāl uṇarappaṭum. añcāteṉpatu īṟu keṭṭu
   niṉṟatu. uyirpperumai, īṇṭuk kuṭippiṟappiṉ mēṟṟu, ivvamutamatiyiṉ
   eṉpatu mutalāyiṉa kuṟippeccam.
   ------------
   kuṇavati eṉṉum tōḻi uṟṟatu viṉātal
   tuṉṉiya iravu nīṅkat tuṇaimulai tamiya ḷāki
       iṉṉicai yavaṉai neñcat tiruttiṉa ḷirunta ellait
       tuṉṉiṉaḷ tōḻi tuṉṉit tuṇaivaril tamiya rēpōṉ
       ṟeṉṉitu niṉainta tuḷḷat tiṟaivinī yaruḷu keṉṟāḷ.         97

   urai:- tuṉṉiya iravu nīṅka-poruntiyirunta irāp pōtu kaḻiya, tuṇaimulai
   tamiyaḷāki-iraṇṭākiya mulaikaḷaiyuṭaiya amutamati ōriṭattē taṉittiruntu
   iṉṉicai yavaṉai-iṉiya icaiyaiyuṭaiyaṉākiya aṭṭa paṅkaṉai, neñcattu
   iruttiṉaḷ-maṉattē kāmak kātalaṉākakkoṇṭu, irunta ellai-vēṟuceyalmaṟantu
   iruntapōtu, tōḻi tuṉṉiṉaḷ-uyirttōḻiyākiya kuṇavati avaḷaiyaṭaintāḷ,
   tuṉṉi-(aṭaintavaḷ) amutamatiyai neruṅki, iraivi-araciyē, tuṇaivaril
   tamiyar pōṉṟu-maṉakkiṉiya kātalar uṭaṉillāmaiyāl taṉittuppulampum
   makaḷiraippōla, itu-vēṟupaṭṭirukkum icceyal, eṉ - eṉṉai, nī uḷḷattu
   niṉaintatu- nī niṉmaṉattil karutiyataṉai, aruḷuka-aṭiyēṉukkut
   terivittaruḷka, eṉṟāḷ-eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉāḷ e-ṟu.
   “tuṉṉiya iravu nīṅka” eṉa vitantōtiyatu, aḵtu avaṭku ōr ūḻiyāyttōṉṟi
   neṭitu kaḻintamai yuṇaraniṉṟatu. yacōtaraṉōṭu kūṭiyiruppavum, neñcilē
   “iṉṉicaiyavaḷai” iruttikkoṇṭamaiyiṉ, avaṉaiyiṉṟi yirukkumatu avaṭkut
   taṉimaiyāyiṟ ṟeṉṟaṟkut “tamiyaḷāki” eṉṟār. yacōtaraṉaiyum
   ēṉaittōḻimārkaḷaiyum nīṅkit taṉittiruntamai tōṉṟa “tamiyaḷāki”
   eṉṟāreṉṟalumām. tuṉṉi yeṉṟa mikaiyāl, kuṉavati pōtarakkaṇṭum avaḷai
   varavēṟkātu tāṉ vāṭiya mukattuṭaṉ cāmpiyiruntamaiyum, avaḷ tiṭukkiṭṭu
   niṉṟu nōkki mella arukaṇaintamaiyum, amutamatiyiṉ maṟukiya neñcamum
   maiyalnōkkamum meyppaṭṭut tōṉṟiṉamaiyum peṟṟām. iṟaivaṉākiya
   yacōtaraṉuṭaṉ piriviṉṟik kūṭiyiruttaliṉ, “iṟaivi” yeṉṟum, pirivāl
   meliyum makaḷirkkuriya mēṉi vēṟupāṭukāṇṭaliṉ, “tuṇaivaril tamiyar
   pōṉṟu” eṉṟum, kāraṇam kāṇamāṭṭāmaiyiṉ, “eṉṉitu” veṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
   ----------
   amirtamati uṟṟatu uraittal
   tavaḷavāḷ nakaiyāy* taṇṭār maṉṉavaṉ takaimai yeṉṉuṅ
       kavaḷamā rakatteṉ ṉuḷḷak karuṅkaḷi matanal liyāṉai
       pavaḷavāy maṇikkai koṇṭa paṇṇiyal tōṭṭi paṟṟit
       tuvaḷumā ṟoruvaṉ ellit tuṭakkiṉāṉ ṟuyara+ eṉṟāḷ.         98
       -----------
       (pāṭam)* tavaḻumāmatiyāy, tavaḻumāmaticey.
       + tuṭaṅkiṉāṉōva; tuṭakkiṉāṉṟuvaḷa.

   urai:- tavaḷavāḷ nakaiyāy - veḷḷiya oḷi poruntiya paṟkaḷai yuṭaiyāy,
   taṇtār maṉṉavaṉ - kuḷirnta mālaiyaṇinta vēntaṉākiya yacōtaraṉuṭaiya,
   takaimai yeṉṉum - kātalaṉ peṉṉum, kavaḷam ār - kavaḷattai yuṇkiṉṟa,
   akattu - iṉpa voḻukkattaiyuṭaiya, eṉ uḷḷam - eṉmaṉamākiya, karuṅkaḷi
   matanalyāṉai - kariya matakkaḷippiṉaiyuṭaiya nallayāṉaiyai, oruvaṉ -
   evaṉō voruvaṉ, pavaḷavāy maṇikkai - pavaḷampōlum taṉ vāyākiya aḻakiya
   kaiyiṭattē, koṇṭa - vaittuḷḷa, paṇiyal tōṭṭi - icaiyākiya aṅkucattāl,
   paṟṟi- kaippaṟṟi, tuvaḷumāṟu - meliyumāṟu, elli -
   nēṟṟiravu,tuyarattuṭakkiṉaṉ - varunta vaṇakkip piṇittuk koṇṭāṉ e-ṟu.
   collāṭaliṉṟit tuyaruṟṟirunta amirtamati cilacollat toṭaṅkiyatum
   kuṇavati mukamalarntu muṟuvalittuk kāṭṭiṉamaiyiṉ, "tavaḷavāṇakaiyāy"
   eṉṟāḷ. ippōtu amutamatiyiṉ uḷḷam yacōtaraṉiṟ pirintu nīṅkiṉamaiyiṉ,
   piriyāmai muṉṉirunta nilaimaiyiṉai, "taṇṭār maṉṉavaṉ takaimai yeṉṉum
   kavaḷam" eṉṟāḷ. kavaḷam, yāṉaiyuṇṉum uṇavu. ārtal, uṇṭal; "ārntōr
   vāyiṟṟēṉum puḷikkum" (kuṟun. 854) eṉpuḻippōla. akam, ākupeyarāy
   iṉpavoḻukkattaik kuṟittu niṉṟatu, icaiyavaṉpāl kaḷaviṉāl kāmaviṉpam
   tuykkak karutukiṉṟāḷātaliṉ, avaḷ uḷḷattaik "karuṅkaḷi matanalyāṉai"
   yeṉṟār. yāṉaiyiṉ karumaiyai uḷḷattukkum koḷka, kaḷaviṉāl tīya kāmam
   nukarak karututaliṉ; "kaḷa veṉṉum kāraṟivāṉmai"1 eṉac cāṉṟōr kūṟutal
   kāṇka. icaiyavaṉ vāyai muṉpu, "paṇkoḷ cevvāy" (95) eṉṟāḷātaliṉ, īṇṭu
   "pavaḷavāy" eṉṟāḷ. yāṉai nallicaikku vaṇaṅkutal paṟṟi, neñciṉai
   yāṉaiyōṭu uruvakam ceytār, uḷḷattai matayāṉai yeṉṟum, ataṉai icaiyāl
   oruvaṉ vaṇakkiṉaṉ eṉṟu kūṟiyataṉāl, yacōtaraṉāl ataṉai vaṇakka
   māṭṭāmaiyum, "iṉṉicaiyavaṉ" vaḻiyē celvataiyum kuṟittāḷāyiṟṟu.
   icaiyicaittavaṉ aṭṭapaṅkaṉ eṉpatu aṟiyāmaiyāl "oruvaṉ" eṉṟāḷ.
   "tuvaḷumāṟu" eṉṟum, "tuyara" veṉṟum kūṟiyatu, avaṉatu iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyum
   taṉatu āṟṟāmaiyum uṇarttiniṉṟatu.
   ----------
       1.kuṟaḷ. 287.

   kaṇṭatu kaṉaveṉak kuṇavati kaḻaṟal
   aṅkava ḷakattuc ceykai yaṟintaṉa ṉalla ḷēpōl*
       koṅkaviḻ kuḻali maṟṟak kuṇavati yuṟaḻntu+ kūṟum
       naṅkainiṉ perumai naṉṟē naṉaveṉak kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭa
       paṅkama tuḷḷi yuḷḷam parivukoṇ ṭiṉaiya‡ leṉṟāḷ.         99
       ---------
       (pāṭam)* ḷallavēpōl. + piṟintu. ‡ṭaṉaiyeṉ.

   urai:- aṅku - avviṭattu, avaḷ akattucceykai - amutamati taṉmaṉattē
   karutiyirukkum ceykaiyai, aṟintaṉaḷ - terintu koṇṭālāyiṉum, allaḷpōla -
   teriyātavaḷpōla, koṅku aviḻ kuḻali akkuṇavati - tēṉporuntiya pūccūṭiya
   kūntalaiyuṭaiyaḷākiya akkuṇavati yeṉṉum tōḻi, uṟaḻntu kūṟum - avaḷōṭu
   māṟu paṭṭukkūṟuvāḷāyiṉaḷ. naṅkai - naṅkaiyē, niṉperumai -
   niṉakkuḷḷaperumaiyō, naṉṟu - peritākum, kaṉaviṉāl - kaṉaviṉkaṇ,
   naṉaveṉak kaṇṭapaṅkamatu-naṉavupōlat tōṉṟak kaṇṭa iḻittakka
   nikaḻcciyai, uḷḷi-niṉaintu, uḷḷam parivukoṇṭu-maṉattil varuttamuṟṟu,
   iṉaiyal- varuntātē koḷ; eṉṟāḷ-eṉṟu uraittāḷ e-ṟu.
   niṉaivu vaṭivākavē niṉṟoḻiyātu ceykaikkaṇṇum oṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ,
   eṇṇameṉṉātu, "avaḷakattuc ceykai" yeṉṟār. amutamatiyiṉ karuttaṟintum
   ataṉ tīmaikaruti vilakkum karut tiṉaḷātaliṉ, "aṟintaṉaḷallaḷēpōl
   uṟaḻntu kūṟum" eṉṟār. "uyarmoḻik kuriya vuṟaḻuṅkiḷavi"1 eṉa āciriyar
   tōḻikkum uritteṉṟaliṉ, tōḻi īṇṭu tēviyai uṟaḻntu kūṟukiṉṟāḷ. naṉṟē
   eṉpuḻi ēkāram etirmaṟaiyākak koṇṭu, itu niṉperumaikkut tītu eṉṟu
   uraippiṉumām. paṅkam, iḻivu; īṇṭu iḻittakka kaṉaviṉmēṟṟu. iṉaivatu
   pētaiyār ceyal eṉpāḷ, "iṉaiyal" eṉṟāḷ. maṟṟu, acainilai.
   --------
       1. tol. poruḷiyal 44.

   amutamati coṟkēṭṭuk kuṇavati añcutal
   eṉmaṉat tivaru minnōy* ivaḷaṟin tilaḷko leṉṟē
       taṉmaṉat tiṉaiya vaṭkut tāṉurait tiṭuta lōṭum
       niṉmaṉat tilāta collai nīpuṉain taruḷiṟ+ ṟeṉkol
       ciṉmalark kuḻali yeṉṟē ceviputait tiṉitu coṉṉāḷ.         100
       -------------
       (pāṭam)* meṉṉō. + niṉaintaruḷiṟṟu.

   urai:- eṉmaṉattu-eṉneñcil, ivarum-mēṉmēṉmikum, innōy-ikkāma nōyai, ivaḷ
   aṟin tilaḷ-ivaḷ iṉṉum aṟiyavillai, eṉṟu-eṉṟu amutamati niṉaittu,
   taṉmaṉattiṉai-taṉ maṉattirunta eṇṇaṅkaḷai, avaṭku-akkuṇavatikku,
   tāṉ-tāṉē vāyviṭṭu, uraittiṭutalōṭum-uraittalum, cilmalark
   kuḻali-cilavākiya pūkkaḷai yaṇinta kūntalaiyuṭaiyāy, niṉmaṉattu
   ilāta-niṉ neñcil illāta iccoṟkaḷai, nīpuṉaintu-nīyē paṭaittukkoṇṭu,
   aruḷiṟṟu-uraittatu, eṉkol-eṉṉai, eṉṟu-eṉṟu colli, cevi putaittu- taṉ
   irukātukaḷaiyum kaikaḷāl pottik koṇṭu, iṉitu coṉṉāḷa-aṟavuraiyaik
   kuṇavati coṉṉāḷ e-ṟu.
   ivartal, mēlum mikuntēkutal, "ivartanteṉ mēṉi mēlurum pacappu"1
   eṉpuḻippōla. kolliraṇṭiṉuḷ muṉṉatu aiyam, piṉṉa tacainilai.
   kulamakaḷirk kovvāta collai amutamati kūṟutaliṉ, "niṉmaṉattu ilāta col"
   eṉṟum, atu meyyaṉ ṟeṉṟaṟku "nī puṉaintaruḷiṟṟu" eṉṟum, atu nakaiyēyum
   vēṇṭaṟpāṟ ṟaṉṟākaliṉ, "eṉkol" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. "puṉainturai yaṉṟu; meyyē"
   eṉa amutamati vaṟpuṟuttiṉamaiyiṉ, kuṇavati poṟātu añciṉamai tōṉṟac
   "ceviputaittu" eṉṟum, aṟavurai pakarntamai viḷaṅka, "iṉitu coṉṉāḷ"
   eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. aṟavurai iṉpam payappatātaliṉ, "iṉi" teṉṟār; avvurai
   aṅkaṇattuḷukka vamiḻtupōl payaṉpaṭā toḻintamaiyiṉ, iṉiteṉṟē yoḻintār.

       1. kuṟaḷ 1182.

   amutamati taṉatu āṟṟāmai kūṟal
   māḷavap pañca vappaṇ makiḻntava ṉamuta vāyiṟ
       kēḷala māyi ṉāmuṅ kēḷala mātu māvi
       nāḷava māki yiṉṉē naṭantiṭu naṭuvoṉ ṟillai
       vāḷaḷa vuṇkaṇ* mātē maṟutturai moḻiyi ṉeṉṟāḷ.†         101
       ------
       (pāṭam)*voṇkaṇ. † moḻiyaleṉṟāḷ.

   urai:- vāḷ aḷavu uṇkaṇ mātē-oḷi kalantamaiyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya aṉpuḷḷa
   tōḻi, avaṉ amutavāyil-anta iṉṉicaiyōṉuṭaiya amutam poruntiya vāyāl,
   māḷavappañcavappaṇ-māḷava pañcamam eṉṟa paṇṇiṉ icaiyai, makiḻntu
   kēḷalamāyiṉ- avaṉaik kūṭi makiḻntu kēḷātoḻivōmāyiṉ, nāmum
   kēḷalamātum-nāmum tōḻamaiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅkiviṭuvōm, maṟuttu uraimoḻiyiṉ-nī
   eṉviruppattai maṟuttu māṟu kūṟuvāyāyiṉ, nāḷ-uyirōṭu kūṭivāḻum vāḻ
   nāḷum, avamāki-payaṉaṟṟatāki, iṉṉē āvinaṭantiṭum- ippoḻutē uyir
   nīṅkiviṭum, naṭu oṉṟu illai- itaṟkiṭaiyē nammai vāḻvippatu vēṟoṉṟum
   kiṭaiyātu e-ṟu.
   maṟutturai moḻiyiṉ, nāḷ avamāki āvi iṉṉē naṭantiṭum eṉa iyait turaittuk
   koḷka. māḷavapañcamam eṉpatu oruvakaip paṇ; māḷava kauḷam, kētāra
   kauḷam eṉpaṉa pōlvatu, iḵtu aṭṭapaṅkaṉ pāṭiyatu. kēḷāvaḻippaṭum payaṉ
   itu veṉpāḷ "nāmum kēḷalamātum" eṉṟum, maṟuttu moḻiyiṉ varum payaṉai
   "āviyiṉṉē naṭantiṭum" eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟāḷ. oṉṟu, nāḷ eṉpuḻic ciṟappummai
   vikārattāl tokkaṉa. vāḷ, oḷi, aḷavu-aḷavutal, kalattal. ivvāṟu
   aṟantiṟampiya neṟiyoḻukupavaḷ kēṇmaiyum uyirvāḻvum peṟutaliṉum
   peṟātoḻivatu peṇṇulakiṟkuc ciṟappāmeṉṟu karuti maṟuppiṉum maṟuppaḷeṉṟa
   eṇṇattāl, varum pāṭṭil kuṇavatiyuṭaṉ mikanayamāka ivvamutamati
   pēcutalaik kāṇmiṉ.
   ---------
   avvicaiyavaṉ yāvaṉeṉ ṟaṟiyak kuṇavati tuṇital
   eṉṉuyirk karaṇam niṉṉō ṭiṉṉicai puṇartta* kāḷai
       taṉṉiṉmaṟ ṟoruva rillait takkatu tuṇika tāḻkkiṉ†
       eṉṉuyirk kēta meyti ṉitupaḻi peruku meṉṟāḷ‡
       tuṉṉumvā yavaḷō ṭeṇṇāt§ tōḻiyu meṇṇi ṇāḷē.         102
       ---------
       (pāṭam)*yuṇartta. † veṉṉa, tāḻviṉ, ++meṉṟā, meṉṟē. $ṭeṇṇi.

   urai:- eṉ uyirkku araṇam-eṉ uyirkkup pātu kāppāvār, niṉṉōṭu-uṉṉōṭu,
   iṉṉicai puṇartta kāḷaitaṉṉiṉ-iṉiya icaiyaippāṭiya akkāḷaiyēyaṉṟi,
   maṟṟu-vēṟē, oruvar illai-oruvarum illai,. takkatu tuṇika-ceyyattakuvatu
   yātō ataṉaic ceyka, tāḻkkiṉ-kālam nīṭṭippiṉ, eṉ uyirkku ētam-eṉ uyirkku
   iṟutiyuṇṭākum, eytiṉ-avvāṟaḵtu uṇṭāyiṉ, itu-ittāḻkkaiyiṉāl, paḻi
   perukum- peṇpaḻi tōṉṟi niṉvaḻivaḻip perukiniṟkum, eṉṟāḷ- eṉṟu
   amutamatiyuraittāḷāka, tōḻiyum-tōḻiyākiya kuṇavatiyum,
   avaḷōṭu-avvamutamatiyuṭaṉ, tuṉṉum vāy-avvicaiyavaṉait tēṭi
   yaṭaitaṟkuriya neṟikaḷai, eṇṇi-ārāyntaṟintu, eṇṇiṉāḷ-avaṉ
   irukkumiṭattait tēṭic cellat tuṇintāḷ e-ṟu.
   "uyirkku araṇamāvār ataṟkuk kēṭu ceyyār" eṉpatu paṟṟi
   "eṉṉuyirkkaraṇamāvār" eṉṟeṭuttu "niṉṉōṭu" eṉṟu uyarpiṉvaḻittākiya
   oṭukkoṭuttuc ciṟappittāḷ; oṭu ōṭeṉa vikāram. tōḻivāyilāka icaiyavaṉaip
   peṟum iyaipu paṟṟi, avaṭku uyarpu tantāḷ eṉa vaṟika. palaraṟiya
   icaiyavaṉait tēṭik kāṇṭal amutamatiyiṉ karuttu niṟaivēṟaṟkākāmaiyiṉ
   "takkatu tuṇika" eṉṟum, ataṉaiyum viraintu ceyalvēṇṭumeṉṟaṟku "tāḻkkiṉ
   ētam" eṉṟum, "paḻiperukum" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. iravuppōtil uṟaṅkuṅkālattē
   avviṉṉicaiyai amutamati kēṭṭāḷātaliṉ, icai vanta pakkam
   icaiyavaṉuṟaiviṭam, ataṉ aṇimai cēymainilai, avaṉaik kāṇuntiṟam, avaṉai
   yuṭaṉpaṭuvittaṟku vēṇṭum coṟporuḷkaḷ eṉṟu iṉṉōraṉṉavaṟṟait tōḻi
   amutamatiyōṭu kalantē yārāyavēṇṭiyiruntamaiyiṉ, "tuṉṉum vāy avaḷō
   ṭeṇṇā" eṉṟum, eṇṇiyapiṉ eṇṇiyāṅkuc ceyvaṉa ceyyat toṭaṅakiṉāḷeṉṟum
   kūṟiṉār. eṇṇuvataṉ payaṉ iyaṟṟalē yātaliṉ, "eṇṇiṉāḷ" eṉṟu upacarittār.
   eṇṇā, ceyyāveṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉaiyeccam.
   -----------
   icai pāṭiyavaṉ aṭṭapaṅkaṉeṉṟu kuṇavati makiḻcciyuṭaṉ mīḷutal
   maḻukiru ḷiraviṉ vaiki māḷavap pañca vattēṉ
       oḻukiya miṭaṟṟōr kāḷai yuḷḷava ṉiyāva ṉeṉṟē
       kaḻuturu vavaṉai* nāṭik kaṇṭaṉaḷ kaṇṭu kāmat
       toḻukiya vuḷḷat taiyaṟ koḻiyumeṉ ṟuvantu mīṇṭāḷ.         103
       ------
       (pāṭam) * vaṉṉai.

   urai:-- iruḷ maḻuku iraviṉ vaiki - naḷḷiruḷ nekiḻum kaṭaiyāmattil taṉ
   irukkaiyilē iruntu, māḷavap pañcavattēṉ - māḷavapañcamam eṉṟa paṇṇākiya
   tēṉai, oḻukiya miṭaṟṟu - corinta kaṇṭattaiyuṭaiya, kāḷaiyuḷḷavaṉ -
   kāḷaipparuvattāṉ, yāvaṉ eṉṟu - yāvaṉō eṉṟu, nāṭi - tēṭic ceṉṟu, kaḻutu
   uru avaṉai- pēyiṉ vaṭivattaiyuṭaiya avaṉai, kaṇṭaṉaḷ - kuṇavati
   taṉkaṇkaḷāl pārttāḷ, kaṇṭu - avvāṟu kaṇṭataṉāl,taiyaṟku - taiyalākiya
   amutamatikku, kāmattu oḻukiya uḷḷam - kaḻikāmattuṟaiyil perukiyōṭum
   maṉamāṉatu, oḻiyum - iṉi ataṉiṉṟum nīṅkum, eṉṟu uvantu - eṉṟu niṉaintu
   makiḻntu, mīṇṭāḷ - amutamatipālvantu cērntāḷ e-ṟu.
   naṭuviyāmattē ceṟintirukkum tiṇiyiruḷ, kaṭaiyāmattē kaṭṭuviṭṭu nekiḻntu
   maḻukiyiruttaliṉ, ataṉai, " maḻukiruḷira viṉ" eṉṟār. avaḷ accamiṉṟic
   cēṟaṟkum atu naṉṟātaliṉ, maḻukiruḷai eṭuttōtiṉār. paṇṇiṉaait tēṉāka
   vuruvakañcey tamaiyiṉ, ataṟkēṟpap pāṭutalai oḻukutaleṉṟār. ōr, acai.
   kāḷaiyuḷḷavaṉ yāvaṉ eṉpataṟku, kāḷaipōlvāṉāy uḷḷāṉ oruvaṉ. avaṉ yāvaṉ
   eṉṟu kūṟalu moṉṟu. kaḻutu - pēy. yāvaṉ eṉṟu nāṭik kaṇṭaṉaḷ eṉa iyaikka.
   kāṇappōnta kuṇavatikku amutamatiyiṉ kāmavoḻukkttiṉkaṇ viruppamiṉmaiyiṉ,
   aṭṭapaṅkaṉatu pēypōlum kāṭci aruvaruppai viḷaittatāyiṉum orupāl mikka
   makiḻcciyē viḷaittateṉpār, ataṟku ētuviṉmēlvaittu "kāmattoḻukiya vuḷḷam
   taiyaṟ koḻiyum eṉṟu uvantu" eṉṟum "mīṇṭāḷ" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. "mīṇṭāḷ"
   eṉṟataṉāl, iṉit taṉakku ittakaiya viṉai nikaḻātu; iḵtu ivvaḷavil
   muṭintoḻintatu eṉṟa karuttuṭaiyaḷāyk kuṇavati tirumpiṉamai peṟṟām.
   eṭutta viṉai muṟṟiṉallatu mīḷutal viṉaiceyvār toḻilaṉṟātaliṉ.
   ---------
   kuṇavati tāṉ kaṇṭataṉai amutamatikkuk kūṟal
   maṉṉaṉmā tēvi niṉṉai varuttuvāṉ vakutta* kītat
       taṉṉava ṉatti pāka ṉaṭṭamā paṅka ṉeṉpāṉ
       taṉṉaimey teriyak kaṇṭē taḷarntukaṇ putaittu mīṇṭēṉ
       eṉṉainī muṉivai yeṉṉā vicaikkila† ṉavaṟkī teṉṟāḷ.         104
       ------
       (pāṭam)*vaḻaṅku. †yeṉṟiḵticaikkalaṉ

   urai:- maṉṉaṉmātēvi-aracamātēvi, niṉṉai varuttuvāṉ- niṉ neñcu
   varuntumpaṭiyāka, vakutta kītattu aṉṉavaṉ - pāṭiya icaiyiṉaiyuṭaiyavaṉ,
   attipākaṉ- ōr yāṉaippākaṉāvāṉ, aṭṭamāpaṅkaṉ eṉpāṉ- aṭṭapaṅkaṉ eṉṟu
   peyar kūṟappaṭuvāṉ, taṉṉai-avaṉai, meyteriyakkaṇṭu-uṭalmuḻutum
   naṉkukaṇṭu, taḷarntu- maṉam cōrntu, kaṇputaittu mīṇṭēṉ-kaṇkaḷai
   mūṭikkoṇṭu tirumpiṉēṉ, eṉṉai nī muṉivai eṉṉā- ceṉṟa karumattai avaṟku
   uraippiṉ eṉṉai nī vekuḷvāy eṉṟu añci, ītu avaṟku icaikkilaṉ-itaṉai
   avaṉukku yāṉ collavillai, eṉṟāḷ-eṉṟu kuṇavatikūṟiṉāḷ e-ṟu.
   maṉṉaṉpāl oṉṟi avaṟku urimaippaṭṭirunta maṉam nīṅki, ayalāṉ oruvaṉmēṟ
   ceṉṟamaiyiṉ, amutamatiyai, aracaṉtēvi yeṉṉātu, "naṅkai" (99) eṉṟum
   "ciṉmalarkkuḻali" (100) eṉṟum kūṟippōntavaḷ, iṉi avvāṟu celvatoḻintu,
   aracaṟkē yurittāṉamaiyiṉ "maṉṉaṉ mātēvi" eṉṟāḷ. aṭṭapaṅkaṉpāl
   taṉakkuṇṭāṉa aruvaruppaik kuṇavati, avaṉicaitta paṇmē lēṟṟi "varuttuvāṉ
   vakutta kītam" eṉṟāḷ. palavēṟupaṇkaḷāka vakukkappaṭutaliṉ, "vakutta
   kītam" eṉṟār. atti, yāṉai. mā, acai. aṭṭapaṅkaṉ vaṭivu muḻutum
   viḷaṅkakkaṇṭamai tōṉṟa, "meyteriyakkaṇṭu" eṉṟum, avvaṭiviṉ pollāṅkiṉait
   "taḷarntu kaṇputaittu" eṉṟu kuṟittum, ataṉaik kāṇiṉ amutamati aṟavē
   avaṉai veṟuppaḷeṉṉum tuṇivāl, "eṉṉai nī muṉivai yeṉṉā vicaikkila
   ṉavaṟkī" teṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. taḷarcci amutamatiyiṉ
   poruṭṭu.
   -----------
   aṭṭapaṅkaṉ vaṭivaik kuṇavati kūṟal
   narampukaḷ vicitta meyyaṉ naṭaiyiṉiṟ kaḻuta ṇaintōṉ
       tiraṅkiya viralaṉ kaiyaṉ ciṟumukaṉ ciṉavaṉ cīṟiṟ*
       kuraṅkiṉai yaṉaiyaṉ+ kūṉaṉ kuḻintupuk kaḻinta kaṇṇaṉ
       neruṅkalu niralu miṉṟi nimirntuḷa cilapal veṉṟāḷ.         105
       ---------
       (pāṭam) *cīṟiṟ. +yaṉaiya.

   urai:-narampukaḷ vicittameyyaṉ - narampukaḷ puṟattē tōṉṟumāṟu eḻunta
   meyyiṉaiyuṭaiyavaṉ, naṭaiyiṉil kaḻutu aṇaintōṉ - naṭaiyāl pēyk
   kōṭpaṭṭāṉai yoppaṉ, tiraṅkiya viralaṉ kaiyaṉ - tōlcuruṅkit tēynta
   viralaiyum kaiyaiyumuṭaiyavaṉ, ciṟumukaṉ - ciṟutta mukattaiyuṭaiyavaṉ,
   ciṉavaṉ - muṉ kōpi, cīṟiṉ kuraṅkiṉaiyaṉaiyaṉ - ciṉantavaḻi mukam
   kuraṅkiṉai yoppaṉ, kūṉaṉ - kūṉiya mutukai yuṭaiyavaṉ, kuḻintupukku -
   kuḻintu uṭceṉṟu, aḻintakaṇṇaṉ - itaḻ puṇṇuṟṟu aḻukiya
   kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiyavaṉ, neruṅkalum niralum iṉṟi - nerukkamum varicaiyumiṉṟi
   aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟumāka, cilapal nimirntu - cilapaṟkaḷē mika nīṇṭu, uḷa -
   avaṉ vāyil uḷḷaṉa, eṉṟāḷ - eṉṟu eṭutturaittāḷ e-ṟu.
   naram peḻuntu vicittatu pōlat tōṉṟaliṉ, "vicitta meyyaṉ" eṉṟāḷ. puḻuti
   yaḷainta mēṉiyum maruṇṭanōkkamum taḷḷāṭunaṭaiyum uṭaimaiyiṉ,
   "naṭaiyiṉiṟ kaḻutaṇaintōṉ" eṉṟāḷ. tiraṅkiya eṉpataṉaik kaiyaṉ eṉpuḻiyum
   kūṭṭuka. talaikkum uṭaṟkum ovvātuciṟuttiruttaliṉ, "ciṟumukaṉ" eṉṟum,
   acciṟumai viraivil vekuḷum iyalpiṉaikkāṭṭaliṉ "ciṉavaṉ" eṉṟum. ciṉantu
   cīṟuṅkāl mukam peṟum vaṭiviṉai "cīṟiṟ kuraṅkiṉai yaṉaiyaṉ" eṉṟum
   kūṟiṉāḷ. kuḻinteṉavē yamaiyumāyiṉum "pukku" eṉṟatu, neṭi tāḻntu
   kiṭaintamai tōṉṟa. itaḻpuṇṇuṟṟu mayirutirntu cīyum pīḷaiyum nīrum
   oḻukutal paṟṟi, "aḻinta kaṇṇaṉ eṉṟāḷ, "cilapal" eṉṟum "nimirntuḷa"
   eṉṟum kūṟiyataṉāl, irukaṭaivāyilum muṉṉum mēlum kīḻum ivviraṇṭu
   paṟkaḷuḷa veṉṟum, muṉṉuḷḷavai mika nīṇṭu puṟattē veḷippaṭṭut tōṉṟiṉa
   veṉṟum koḷka.
   --------
   iṉṉa iyalpiṉaṉāṉa ivaṉai nī vēṇṭaṟkaeṉak kuṇavati kūṟal
   pūtikan tattiṉ meyyiṟ puṇkaḷuṅ kaṇkaḷ koḷḷā
       cātiyun takka taṉṟā lavaṉvayiṟ ṟaḷaru muḷḷam
       nītavirn tiṭuka neñciṉ niṟaiyiṉaic ciṟaicey keṉṟāḷ
       kōtavitaḻn tiṭṭa vuḷḷak kuṇavati kompa ṉāḷē.         106

   urai:- pūti kantattiṉ-puḻuti nāṟṟamum, meyyiṟ puṇkaḷum-uṭaliṭattēyuḷḷa
   puṇkaḷum, kaṇkaḷ koḷḷā-kaṇkaḷtāmum pārttaṟku uṭaṉpaṭā, cātiyum
   takkataṉṟu-avaṉuṭaiya kuṭippiṟappum takutiyuṭaiyataṉṟu, avaṉ vayiṉ
   taḷarum uḷḷam-avaṉ poruṭṭu varuntimeliyum niṉmaṉa viruppattai, nī
   tavirntiṭuka-nī viṭṭoḻittu, neñciṉ-avaṉpāṟcellum niṉ neñcattai,
   niṟaiyiṉai-niṉakku iyalpāyuḷḷa niṟaiyaik koṇṭu, ciṟaiceyka-taṭuttu
   niṟuttik koḷka, eṉṟāḷ-eṉṟu parivōṭu kūṟiṉāḷ. kōtu aviḻntiṭṭa-kuṟṟam
   illāta, uḷḷak kuṇavati-maṉattaiyuṭaiya kuṇavati yeṉṉum,
   kompaṉṉāḷ-pūṅkompu pōlum tōḻi e-ṟu.
   pūti-puḻuti. kantam-nāṟṟam. kāṇṭaṟku nam neñcu orukāl virumpiṉum,
   karuviyākiya kaṇkaḷ ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭā eṉap pārttaṟku mikka
   aruvaruppuṭaiya avaṉatu uṭal vaṭivai yuṇarttuvāḷ "kaṇkaḷ koḷḷā" eṉṟāḷ.
   meyyiṟ puṇ kūṟavē, av vaṭṭapaṅkaṉ kuṭṭa nōyāl varuntutal peṟṟām. cāti,
   īṇṭu iṟpiṟappiṉ mēṟṟu. neñciṉ eṉpuḻi aiyurupu, cāriyai niṟkat
   tāṉnilaiyātāyiṟṟu. niṟaiyiṉai yeṉ puḻik koṇṭu eṉṟu orucol varuvikka.
   niṟaiyiṉai yeṉpuḻi aiyurupaip pirittu neñcōṭu kūṭṭi, neñciṉai
   niṟaiyiṉāl ciṟai ceyka eṉṟuraippiṉumām. iṉi, neñcu il niṟaiyiṉaic ciṟai
   ceyka eṉṟu koṇṭu, neñciṭattē nillā toḻukum niṉ niṟaiyiṉai niṟuttuc
   ciṟai ceykai eṉiṉumamaiyum. kōtu, kuṟṟam. kuṇavatiyaik "kompaṉāḷ" eṉa
   vicēṭittataṉāl, amutamatiyākiya pūṅkoṭikkuk koḻu kompāy niṉṟu, akkoṭi
   paṭara virumpum iṭattiṟkuk kompu tuṇaiceytu niṟkumāṟu pōla, amutamati
   virumpum aṭṭa paṅkaṉai aṭaintu makiḻtaṟku ikkuṇavati tuṇaiyāvatu
   peṟṟām.
   ----------
   kuṇavati kūṟiyavaṟṟai amutamati mēṟkoḷḷātu maṟuttal.
   eṉṟalu mivaṟṟi ṉāleṉ ṉiṟaivaḷai yavaṉka ṇārvam
       ceṉṟatu ciṟantu muṉṉē tiruvoṭu tiṟalun tēcum
       oṉṟiya aḻakuṅ* kalvi yoḷiyamai kulattō ṭellām
       niṉṟucey payaṉu nallār neñcamum peṟuta laṉṟō.         107
       ------
       (pāṭam) vaṭivuṅ.

   urai:- eṉṟalum-eṉṟu kuṇavati kūṟiṉatum, iṟaivaḷai-iṟaiporuntiya
   vaḷaiyuṭaiyāy, ivaṟṟiṉāl eṉ-nī kūṟiya ikkuṟaipāṭukaḷāl varum kēṭu
   eṉṉai, avaṉkaṇ ārvam ciṟantu muṉṉē ceṉṟatu-avvaṭṭa paṅkaṉpāl eṉ neñcam
   kāmakkātal mikuntu muṉṉamē ceṉṟoṭuṅkiviṭṭatu.
   tiruvoṭutiṟalum-celvattōṭu kūṭiya valiyum, tēcum-pukaḻum, oṉṟiya
   aḻakum- uṭalōṭu oṉṟupaṭṭut tōṉṟum vaṉappum, kalvi-kalviyaṟivum,
   oḷiyamai kulattōṭu-pukaḻniṟainta kuṭipiṟappōṭu, ellām-kūṟappaṭāta
   piṟavumellām, niṉṟu- eñcāmal niṉṟu.ceypayaṉum-oruvarkkuviḷaikkakkūṭiya
   payaṉtāṉum. nallār neñcamum-aḻakiya makaḷiriṉ maṉamum ataṉ vāyilāka
   eytum avaratu pōkamum, peṟutal-peṟuvatām, eṉṟu colli mēlum kūṟukiṉṟāḷ
   e-ṟu.
   kuṇavati kūṟiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷaik kēṭṭa amutamati, avaḷai maṟukkumāṟṟāl,
   kuṇameṉak karutappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟaik kūṟaluṟṟu, tiruvum,
   tiṟalum, tēcum, aḻakum. kalviyum, oḷiyum, kulaṉum, piṟavumellām oṉṟi
   niṉṟu oruvarkkuc ceyyum payaṉ aḻakiya makaḷir neñcum avar tarum pōkamum
   peṟutalēyām; peṟāvaḻi avaiyum kuṟṟamām; kuṟṟameṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭaṉatāmum
   appayaṉai eytuvikkumāyiṉ, kuṇamām eṉpatu kuṟippāl uraittāḷāyiṟṟu. ellām
   eṉṟatu, kūṟappaṭāta kuṇamellām eñcāmal taḻuvi niṉṟatu. payaṉum eṉpuḻi
   ummai ciṟappu; neñcamum eṉpuḻi ummai, eccap poruṭṭu. piṟa eṇṇupporuḷil
   vantaṉa. aṉṟō, acai.
   -----------
   kāriyam muṭinta piṉṉuṅ kāraṇa muṭivu kāṇal
       kāriya maṉṟi teṉṟē karutiṭu kaṭavuṭ kāmaṉ
       yāruḻai yaruḷaic ceyyu mavaṉamak kaiya ṉāka.*
       nēriḻai niṉaintu pōki nīṭalai muṭiyi teṉṟāḷ.         108
       -----------
       (pāṭam) *kaṉaiyaṉāka.

   urai:- nēriḻai-nēriya iḻaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya tōḻi. kāriyam muṭinta
   piṉṉum-oruvaṉ eṭutta kāriyam muṭinta piṉṉarum, kāraṇa muṭivu
   kāṇal-akkāriyam toṭaṅku muṉ kāṇavēṇṭiya kāraṇamum payaṉum ārāytal,
   kāriyamaṉṟu eṉṟu-ceyyattakuvataṉṟu eṉṟum, itu eṉṟum-ituvum atupōlvatē
   yeṉṟum. karutiṭu- uṇarvāyāka, kaṭavuḷ kāmaṉ-kaṭavuḷākiya kāmatēvaṉ,
   yāruḻai aruḷaic ceyyum-evaṉpāl taṉ aruḷaic ceykiṉṟāṉō, avaṉ namakku
   aiyaṉ-avaṉē namakkuk kāmak kiḻavaṉāvāṉ, āka,-ātalāl, niṉaintupōki-
   itaṉai niṉaittukkoṇṭu ceṉṟa, nīṭalai-nīṭṭiyātu, itu muṭi-itaṉai
   muṭittukkoṇṭu varuka, eṉṟāḷ- eṉṟu amutamati coṉṉāḷ eṟu.
   oruvaṉ oru kāriyattaic ceyya vēṇṭiṉ, toṭakkattē akkāriyattukkuriya
   kāraṇaṅkaḷaiyum avaṟṟāl atu muṭivataiyum, ataṉālām payaṉaiyum cīrtūkkik
   kāṇṭal vēṇṭu meṉṟum, muṭintapiṉ kāṇṭalāl oru payaṉum viḷaiyāteṉṟum
   poruḷ nūl kūṟutaliṉ, "kāriya muṭinta piṉṉum kāraṇa muṭivu kāṇal, kāriya
   maṉṟu" eṉṟāḷ. eṉṟu eṉpataṉai aṉṟeṉpataṉōṭum kūṭṭuka. itu veṉpuḻi
   eccavummaiyai virittu aṉṉatē eṉa oru col varuvittu muṭikka. iṉi, tāṉ
   mēṟkoṇṭirukkum ceyaṟku ōr amaiti kūṟuvāḷāy, makaḷir āṭavarpāl
   kāmaviṉpan tuyttalē poruḷākavuṭaiya reṉṟum, akkāmaviṉpattai niyamikkum
   kāmak kaṭavuḷ yāvarpāl ataṉaik kūṭṭukiṉṟāṉō, avarpāl am makaḷir ataṉaip
   peṟaṟpālar eṉṟum colvāḷ, "kaṭavuṭ kāmaṉ yāruḻai aruḷaic ceyyum avaṉ
   namakku aiyaṉ" eṉṟāḷ. muṉpu vēṟu niṉaivoṭu ceṉṟamaiyiṉ muṭittilai;
   ippōtu ikkaruttai niṉaintu ceṉṟu muṭikka veṉpāḷ, "niṉaintu pōki" eṉṟum,
   viraintuceytal vēṇṭumeṉṟaṟku, "nīṭalai" yeṉṟum, muṭittukkoṇṭaṉṟi vaṟitu
   mīḷalākā teṉpāḷ "muṭi" yeṉṟum viraivutōṉṟa "yitu" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
   -----------
   kuṇavati maṉam naṭuṅki niṟṟal
   tēvinī kamalai yāvāy tiruvuḷat taruḷap paṭṭāṉ
       āvicel kiṉṟa vennō yarunavai ñamali yākum
       pūviṉvār kaṇaiya ṉeṉṉē puṇarttavā ṟitaṉai yeṉṉā
       nāviṉā luḷaintu kūṟi naṭuṅkupu voṭuṅki niṉṟāḷ.         109

   urai:- tēvi-aracamātēvi, nī kamalaiyāvāy- nī tāmaraimēlirukkum
   tirumakaḷai yoppāy, tiruvuḷattu- niṉ aḻakiya maṉattāl,
   aruḷappaṭṭāṉ-virumpap paṭṭavaṉō, āvi celkiṉṟa-uyir pōtaṟkētuvākiya,
   vennōy arunavai-koṭiya kuṭṭanōyāl uṟṟa tāṅkamuṭiyāta tuṉpattaiyuṭaiya,
   ñamaliyākum-nāyiṉai yoppāṉ, pūviṉvār kaṇaiyaṉ-pūvākiya nīṇṭa
   ampiṉaiyuṭaiya kāmakkaṭavuḷ, itaṉaip puṇarttavāṟu- niṉṉai avaṉukku
   niyamitta vakaitāṉ, eṉṉē-eṉṉeṉpatu, eṉṟu naṭuṅkupu-eṉṟu maṉaṅkalaṅki,
   nāviṉāl uḷaintu kūṟi-nāvāl varuntic colli, oṭuṅki niṉṟāḷ- uṭal curuṅki
   niṉṟoḻintāḷ. e-ṟu.
   kamalai, kamalattai irukkaiyākavuṭaiyavaḷ; kamalam-tāmarai. puṇṇiya
   muṭaiyārai yaruḷuvatē tirumakaḷiṉaṉ uḷappaṇpu eṉṟataṟkut "tiruvuḷam"
   eṉṟum, niṉ uḷattāl virumpap paṭṭavaṉ aṉṉaṉākātu perum pāvattiṉ payaṉāka
   veytum kuṭṭa nōyum koṭun tuṉpamum uṭaiyāṉeṉpāḷ, "aruḷap paṭṭāṉ āvi
   celkiṉṟa vennōy arunavai ñamaliyākum" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. aruvaruppu mikuti
   tōṉṟaṟku, "āvi celkiṉṟa vennōy" eṉṟāḷ. "pūviṉvār kaṇaiyaṉ itaṉaip
   puṇarttavāṟu eṉṉē" eṉṟatu, tirumakaḷai oru nāyiṭattē kāmaviṉpan
   tuykkumāṟu niyamikkum kāmakkaṭavuḷai ikaḻntavāṟām. ivvāṟu
   kāmakkaṭavuḷai ikaḻntum, tēviyiṉ ceykaiyai veṟuttum, tāṉ avaḷ
   ceykaikkut tuṇaiyāka nērntataṟku varuntiyum veṟupput tōṉṟum coṟkaḷai
   vaḻaṅkutaliṉ, "nāviṉāluḷaintu kūṟi" yeṉṟum, piṉviḷaivu niṉaintu añci
   uḷaṅkulaivatu tōṉṟa, "naṭuṅkupu voṭuṅki niṉṟāṉ" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
   ---------
   kuṇavati aṭṭapaṅkaṉiṭam cellutal
   āṭava raṉṟi mēlā raruvarut* taṇaṅka ṉāruṅ†
       kūṭalar tuṟantār‡ nōṉmaik kuṇampurin tuyartaṟ kākap
       pīṭuṭai yayaṉār tanta perumaka ḷivaḷeṉ ṟeṇṇit§
       tōṭalar kuḻali tōḻi tuṇintaṉaḷ peyarttuc ceṉṟāḷ.         110
       -------
       (pāṭam)* mēṉāḷaruntavat. †taṉaṅkaṉārai, taṉaṅkaṉārum, taṇaṅkaṉārum.
       ‡tuṟantu, §ṟuḷḷē.

   urai:-aṇaṅkaṉārum-tirumakaḷai yotta makaḷir tāmum, āṭavariṉṟi-tām
   virumpum āṇmakkaḷai yaṉṟi mēlār-maṇantu kūṭutaṟkamaintavar mikka
   mēlōrāyiṉum, aruvaruttu-avarai veṟuttu, kūṭalar-kūṭuva toḻittu, nōṉmaik
   kuṇam purintu-tavamum viratamum mēṟkoṇṭu, uyartaṟkāka-tēvarāyt tām
   viḻainta pōkattait tuyppatu kuṟittu, tuṟantaṉar-tuṟavu pūṇṭaṉar,
   pīṭuṭai ayaṉār-perumai poruntiya piramaṉāl, tanta-paṭaikkappaṭṭa,
   perumakaḷ ivaḷ-perumaiyiṉai yuṭaiyavaḷ ivvamutavati, ātaliṉ ivaḷ
   tuṟattaliṉ iṟattalē tavaṟātu ceyvaḷ, eṉṟu eṇṇi - eṉṟu taṉakkuḷḷē
   ārāyntu, tuṇintaṉaḷ-aṭṭapaṅkaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu tēvi virumpiya kāriyattai
   muṭittuttara maṉam tuṇintu, peyarttu-maṟupaṭiyum, tōṭu alar kuḻali
   tōḻi-pūkkaḷaiyaṇinta kūntalaiyuṭaiya amutamatikkut tōḻiyākiya kuṇavati,
   ceṉṟāḷ-aṭṭapaṅkaṉ irukkumiṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟāḷ e-ṟu.
   āṭavar, meyvaṉmaiyum iḷamaic cevviyum uṭaiya āṇ makkaḷ. ummai, ciṟappu.
   ettuṇai mēlōrāyiṉum tam maṉam cellāvaḻi makaḷir aruvaruppareṉpatu
   ulakaṟinta ceyalātaliṉ, "aruvaruttu aṇaṅkaṉārum kūṭalar" eṉṟum, avar
   karuttaṟiyātu ammēlōraip puṇarttavaḻi ammakaḷir ceyyum ceykai kūṟuvāḷ
   "tuṟantār nōṉmaikkuṇam purintuyartaṟkāka" eṉṟum, aḻakum tiruvum
   iḷamaiyum aṭaiya mikkiruttaliṉ, piramaṉ ciṟappāka muyaṉṟu paṭaitta
   perumaiyuṭaiyavaḷ ivvamutamati yeṉṟaṟku "pīṭuṭaiyayaṉār tanta
   perumakaḷ" eṉṟum, kaṇavaṉōṭu kūṭiyiruttaliṉ, tuṟavu pūṇpatai viṭuttu
   iṟat talaiyē ceyvaḷ eṉpāḷ "perumakaḷ ivaḷ" eṉṟum kuṇavati
   eṇṇiṉāḷāyiṟṟu. "iṟappaḷ" eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟilaḷ, neñciṉuḷ ataṉait teḷiya
   vuṇarntu koṇṭamaiyiṉ. kuṇavatiyum amutamatiyiṉ karuttukku nērntu
   uṭampaṭṭamaiyiṉ "tōṭalar kuḻali tōḻi" eṉṟum, tuṇiyātiruntavaḷ, ippōtu
   aṭṭapaṅkaṉpāl celvatu takkatu eṉṟu tuṇintāḷeṉpār "tuṇintaṉaḷ" eṉṟum,
   iṭaiyaṟaviṉṟi uṭaṉē cellutal tōṉṟa, "tuṇintaṉaḷ ceṉṟāḷ" eṉṟum,
   muṉṉorukāṟ ceṉṟiruttaliṉ, "peyarttu" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. nōṉmai, tavam.
   kuṇam, viratam,. tavamuṭaiyārkku viratam paṇpātaliṉ, ataṉaik "kuṇam"
   eṉṟār. cīvakaṉai maṇakkak karutiyirunta kuṇamālaikku vēṟṟu varaivu
   pēcappaṭṭapōtu, "kāḷaikku entaiyum yāyum nērāviṭiṉ iṟattaloṉṟō,
   cintaṉai piṟitoṉṟākic cey tavam muyaṟaloṉṟō"1 eṉak kūṟutal kāṇka.
   -------
       1.cīvaka. 1057

   camaṇ camayattavar, makaḷirkku vīṭupēṟu illai eṉpavākaliṉ, uyartal
   eṉpataṟkut tēvarāy iṉpuṟutal eṉṟu urai kūṟappaṭṭatu. "viratacī
   lattarākit tāṉameyt tavarkkuc ceytu, arukaṉa caraṇa mūḻki yāṉṟavarc
   ciṟappuc ceytu, karutu naṟ kaṇavaṟ pēṇuṅ kaṟpuṭai makaḷi rinta,
   uruvatti ṉīṅkik kaṟpat tuttama tēva rāvār" (739) eṉṟum "mātavan tāṅki
   vaiyattaiyarāy vantu tōṉṟi, ētamoṉṟiṉṟi vīṭu meytuvar" (740) eṉṟum
   mērumantara purāṇam kūṟutal kāṇka. tēvar peṟum iṉpattai,
   ”āṭātu moḷitikaḻu māraṇaṅku tirumēṉi
       vāṭāta kaṇṇiyiṉar maḻuṅkāta pūntukilar
       ēṭārnta toṅkalarāy iṉpanīrp peruveḷḷam
       nīṭārak kuḷittāṭum nilaimaiyarē yavarellām" (tuṟavu 211)

   eṉat tōlāmoḻit tēvar kūṟuvatu kāṇka.

   kuṇavati aṭṭapaṅkaṉpāl tēviyiṉ viḻaivait terivittal
   *
   muṭaippaṭu nāṟṟa mēṉi muḻutaḻuk kāṭai pōrttuk
       kaṭaippaṭu tukaḷu maṇṇuṅ kañaliya kūṭat tāṅkōr
       puṭaikkiṭan tuṟaṅku vāṉaip puḻuṅkiya maṉattō ṭaṇmi
       viṭaipparun tāṉai vēntaṉ ṟēviyiṉ viḻaivu coṉṉāḷ.         111

   ---------
   *itumutal aintu pāṭṭukkaḷ accuppiratiyil illai.
   urai:- muṭaippaṭu nāṟṟam mēṉi-muṭaināṟṟam nāṟukiṉṟa vuṭampu,
   muḻutu-muḻutum, aḻukkāṭai pōrttu-aḻukkēṟiya āṭaiyāl pōrttukkoṇṭu,
   kaṭaippaṭutukaḷum-tūymaiyaṟṟatākak karutappaṭum kūḷamum,
   maṇṇum-maṇpoṭiyum, kañaliya kūṭattu- niṟainta yāṉaik kūṭattil, ōr
   puṭai-oru puṟattē, kiṭantu uṟaṅkuvāṉai-paṭuttuṟaṅkiya aṭṭapaṅkaṉai,
   puḻuṅkiya maṉattōṭu aṇmi-aruvarutta maṉattuṭaṉ arukē ceṉṟu, viṭaippu
   arun tāṉai vēntaṉ tēviyiṉ viḻaivu-vēṟupaṭuttaṟkariya tāṉaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya
   vēntaṉāṉa yacōtaraṉukku maṉaiviyākiya amutamatiyiṉ viruppattai,
   coṉṉāḷ-cilavāya coṟkaḷāl kuṇavati mellac coṉṉāḷ e-ṟu.
   mēṉi muḻutum puṇṇuṟṟut tīnāṟṟam nāṟak kiṭattaliṉ, "muṭaippaṭu nāṟṟa
   mēṉi muḻutum" eṉṟār. "meyyiṟ puṇkaḷum kaṇkaḷ koḷḷā" (106) eṉṟu
   kuṇavatiyum, "puṇ peṟṟa meyyaṉ" (113) eṉṟu avaṉēyum kūṟutal kāṇka.
   muṭaippaṭu nāṟṟam, muṭaiyuṇṭākum nāṟṟam, cīvaṭitaliṉ ī mutaliyaṉa
   moykkāvaṇṇam pōrttuk kiṭantāṉ eṉpār "mēṉi muḻutum aḻukkāṭai pōrttu"
   eṉṟār. yāṉai tiṉṟu kaḻinta puṟṟukaḷum vaittukaḷum ataṉ ciṟunīrāl
   naṉaintu ataṉ malam aḷaintu pularntu kiṭattaliṉ "kaṭaippaṭutuka"
   ḷeṉṟār. kāṇṭaṟkut tuṇintu pōntāḷāyiṉum, avaṉatu mēṉiyum, āṭaip
   pōrvaiyum iṭattiṉ iḻivum kāṇakkāṇa avaṭku aruvaruppu neñci
   leḻuntamaiyiṉ "puḻuṅkiya maṉattōṭu" aṇukiṉāḷ. pakaivarāl
   vēṟupaṭuttaṟkariya tāṉaiyātaliṉ, viṭaipparun tāṉaiyāyiṟṟu. "viṭaipparun
   tāṉai vēntaṉ vēṇṭuva veṟuppa nalki"1 eṉṟu tēvarum kūṟutal kāṇka.
   cilavāya coṟkaḷāl tam karuttai mellac collutal iḷaiya makaḷirkku
   iyalpātaliṉ, ataṉai eṭuttōtātu "viḻaivu coṉṉāḷ." eṉṟoḻintār. aṉṟi,
   eṭuttōtaṟku nāṇiyatu tōṉṟa uyttuṇara vaittāreṉiṉumām.
   vēṟupaṭuttaṟkariya tāṉai koṇṭu nāṭṭukku vēṇṭum kāvalai naṉku ceyyum
   vēntaṉāyiṉum, avaṉ tēvikku akkāval payaṉpaṭātāyiṟṟeṉpār,
   "viṭaipparuntāṉai vēntaṉ tēvi" eṉṟār.
   -------------
       1. cīvaka. 557

   aṭṭapaṅkaṉ uṭaṉpaṭātu vekuḷutal
   kēṭṭalu mañcu neñcaṉ kēṭeṉak keytiṟ ṟeṉṟē
       vāṭṭamum naṭukku muṟṟē māṇpila moḻintāy maṉṉaṉ
       vīṭṭiṭuñ celkeṉ ṟēva vēypurai tōḷi tōḻi
       kāṭṭuva kāṭṭi yaṉṉāṉ karuttaiyuṅ kalaittiṭ ṭāḷē.         112

   urai:- kēṭṭalum-kuṇavati colliyatu kēṭṭamāttiraiyē, añcum
   neñcaṉ-añcukiṉṟa maṉamuṭaiyavaṉāṉa aṭṭapaṅkaṉ, eṉakkuk kēṭu eytiṟṟu
   eṉṟu- eṉakku nācakālam vantatu eṉṟu niṉaittu, vāṭṭamum naṭukkum uṟṟu-
   maṉavāṭṭamum naṭukkamumaṭaintu, māṇpu ila moḻintāy-tēviyiṉ perumaikkut
   takātavaṟṟaic colkiṉṟāy, maṉṉaṉ vīṭṭiṭum-itaṉai yaṟintāl vēntaṉ
   koṉṟuviṭuvāṉ, celka-nī ippōtē ivviṭattiṉiṉṟum pōyviṭuka, eṉṟu ēva-eṉṟu
   ciṉanta kūṟa, vēypurai tōḷi tōḻi-mūṅkilpōlum tōḷkaḷaiyuṭaiya
   amutamatiyiṉ tōḻiyākiya kuṇavati, kāṭṭuva kāṭṭi-avaṉ aiyuṟātu
   ēṟkattakka cāṉṟukaḷai eṭuttuk kūṟikkāṭṭi, aṉṉāṉ karuttaiyum
   kalaittiṭṭāḷ-avaṉuṭaiya maṉakkaruttaik kalaittut tēviyiṉ viruppattukku
   icaivittukkoṇṭāḷ e-ṟu.
   iyalpākavē accamuṭaiyaṉātaliṉ, kuṇavati kūṟiyataṉāl atu mikuntu
   varuttuvatupaṟṟi "añcum neñcaṉ" eṉṟār. "accamē kīḻkaḷatu ācāram"1
   eṉpataṉāl, accam aṭṭapaṅkaṟku iyalpātalaṟika. aracaṉ kōyiliṟ
   paṇipurivōr aṭaṅkiya maṉamilarāyiṉ, kēṭeytal oru talaiyātaliṉ, "kēṭeṉak
   keytumeṉṟu vāṭṭamam naṭukkamum" koṇṭāṉ. "maṉṉar kōyil uṟaivār poṟi
   ceṟitta māṇpiṉarē"2 eṉṟu tiruttakka tēvar kūṟutal kāṇka. tēvikkut
   tōḻiyāvāḷ avaḷ māṇpiṟku iḻukkut taruvataṉaic colvatu tītātaliṉ "māṇ
   pila moḻintāy" eṉṟum, avaḷai accuṟuttip pōkkum karuttāl "maṉṉaṉ
   vīṭṭiṭum celka" eṉṟum, eṉṟataṉōṭamaiyātu, avaṉ collakkēṭṭum kuṇavati
   puṭaipeyarāmaiyiṉ, avaṉē avaḷaip pōkkak karutiṉāṉeṉpār "ēva" eṉṟum
   kūṟiṉār. vēypurai tōḷi tōḻi yeṉṟatu, tāṉ karutiyatu muṭittukkoṇṭallatu
   ēkāta iyalpiṉaḷātalai yuṇarttiṟṟu. ivvāṟu avaṉ maṉantiṟampāṉātalaik
   kaṇṭa kuṇavati avaṉ ēṟkattakka coṟkaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṟkuc cāṉṟākak kāṭṭat
   takuvaṉavum vaḻaṅki, avaṉai matiyuṭampaṭuttāḷ eṉpār, "kāṭṭuva kāṭṭi
   yaṉṉāṉ karuttaiyum kalaittiṭṭāḷē" eṉṟār. pētaiyātaliṉ, piṟaṉil viḻaiyum
   it tīviṉaikku avaṉum icaintāṉeṉa vaṟika.
   ----------
       1.kuṟaḷ.77. 2. cīvaka. 1676

   aṭṭapaṅkaṉ maṉaṅkalaintu makiḻnturaittal
   paṇpeṟṟa moḻiyāy yāṉum paṇpeṟṟa payaṉum peṟṟēṉ
       puṇpeṟṟa meyyaṉ pollāp puḻutiyiṟ ṟuḷaiyuṅ kaiyaṉ
       maṇpeṟṟa iṟaivaṉ tēvi maṉampeṟṟu makiḻvē ṉeṉṉiṉ
       eṇpeṟṟa tavamiyār peṟṟā riyāṉpeṟṟa pēṟi teṉṟāṉ.         113

   urai:- paṇ peṟṟa moḻiyāy-paṇṇicaipōlum coṟkaḷaiyuṭaiyavaḷē, paṇpeṟṟa
   payaṉ-paṇpāṭum aṟivu peṟṟataṉālaṭaiyum payaṉai, yāṉum-peṟaṟkākāta
   yāṉum, peṟṟēṉ-iṉṟu peṟṟēṉ, (evvāṟeṉil) puṇpeṟṟa meyyaṉ- puṇkaḷ niṟainta
   uṭampiṉaiyuṭaiyēṉ, pollāp puḻutiyil-nallatallāta puḻutiyilē,
   tuḷaiyumkaiyaṉ- puraṇṭukiṭakkum ciṟumaiyumuṭaiyēṉ, maṇpeṟṟa iṟaivaṉ
   tēvi-maṇṇulakattaiyāḷum aracaṉukku maṉaiviyiṉuṭaiya, maṉam peṟṟu
   makiḻvēṉ-kātalaṉpu peṟṟu makiḻkiṉṟēṉātalāl, eṇpeṟṟa tavam-yāvarum eṇṇi
   virumpattakka tavap payaṉai, eṉṉiṉ-eṉṉaippōla, yār peṟṟār-yāvar peṟṟār.
   itu yāṉ peṟṟa pēṟu-itu yāṉ aritākap peṟṟa pēṟākum, eṉṟāṉ-eṉṟu
   aṭṭapaṅkaṉ kuṇavatikkuk kūṟiṉāṉ. e-ṟu.
   ummai, ciṟappu. "puṇpeṟṟa meyyaṉ", "puḻutiyiṟṟuḷaiyuṅ kaiyaṉ", eṉat taṉ
   nilaimaiyai yuṇarntiruttaliṉ, "yāṉum pēṟṟēṉ" eṉṟum, toṭakkattē
   paṇpilavāyt tōṉṟiya moḻikaḷ iṟutiyil iṉpap payaṉ tantamaiyiṉ, "paṇpeṟṟa
   moḻiyāy" eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. taṉ nilaimaiyai nōkka aracamātēviyiṉ iṉpappēṟu
   taṉakku vāyttataṟkut taṉ tavamēyaṉṟip piṟitoru kāraṇamum
   kāṇamāṭṭāmaiyiṉ, "eṉṉiṉ eṇ peṟṟa tavamiyār peṟṟār" eṉṟāṉ. aritiṟ peṟṟa
   pēṟu eṉa viyanturaittaliṉ "yāṉ peṟṟa pēṟitu" eṉa vitantōtappaṭṭatu.
   ----------
   kuṇavati pōntu araciyaik kāṇṭal
   ivvakai moḻivōṉ ṟaṉṉai yiṟaiyavaṉ tēvi mēvum
       cevviyuṅ kuṟiyuñ ceppic ceṉṟava ḷeyta lōṭum
       kavviya kāmat tīyāṟ kayaṅkiya mālai yoppāḷ
       navvinēr viḻiyāy naṉṟō navilkaniṉ karuma meṉṟāḷ.         114

   urai:- ivvakai moḻivōṉ taṉṉai- ivvāṟu colli makiḻum aṭṭapaṅkaṟku,
   iṟaiyavaṉ tēvi- aracaṉ maṉaiviyākiya amutamati, mēvum cevviyum kuṟiyum
   ceppi-varakkūṭiya kālamum iṭamum uraittuviṭṭu, ceṉṟavaḷ-tēvipāṟ ceṉṟa
   kuṇavati, eytalōṭum- amutamatiyai nōkki yaṇukavarutalum, kavviya kāmat
   tīyāl-paṟṟi varuttum kāmattīyiṉāl.kayaṅkiya mālai oppāḷ-cāmpiya
   pūmālaiyai yoppavaḷāṉa amirtamati, navvinēr viḻiyāy-māṉpōlum
   kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiyāy, niṉ karumam-nī mēṟkoṇṭu ceṉṟa kāriyam, naṉṟō-nalan
   tantatō, navilka-colluka, eṉṟāḷ-eṉṟu kēṭṭāḷ e-ṟu.
   cevvi, kālam. kuṟi,kuṟikkum iṭam. "ceppi" yeṉṟataṉāl, avaṟṟai yavaṉ
   viṉaviṉamai peṟṟām. yāṉaik kūṭattiṉ nīṅki amirtamati yirunta
   antappurattai yaṭaintāḷeṉṟaṟku, "ceṉṟavaḷ eytalōṭum" eṉṟum, kuṇavati
   mēṟkoṇṭu ceṉṟa kāriyam muṭiyumō muṭiyātō eṉṟa aiyam oru puṭai yalaippa,
   avaṉpāṟ ceṉṟa kāmavēṭkai orupuṭai yalaippa uṇarvum uṭampum
   vāṭiyiruttaliṉ, "kavviya kāmat tīyāl kayaṅkiya mālai yoppāḷ" eṉṟār.
   tītō eṉṉāmai, ataṉaiyavaḷ virumpāmaiyālām.
   ------------
   kuṇavati nikaḻntatu kūṟakkēṭṭu makiḻtal
   paṭṭatu nalaṅkā ṇeṉpāḷ pākaṉaik kaṇṭa vāṟuma
       aṭṭamā paṅkaṉ cīṟi yaḻaṉṟiṭṭa vāṟun tēvi
       iṭṭattiṟ kavaṉai yāṅkē yiyaivitta vāṟuṅ kūṭaṟ
       koṭṭiya kuṟiyuṅ coṉṉā ḷoḷḷiḻai yuvappuṟ ṟāḷē.         115

   urai:- nalampaṭṭatu eṉpāḷ-nalamākavē muṭintatu eṉṟu collit toṭaṅkiya
   tōḻi, pākaṉaik kaṇṭavāṟum-tāṉ ceṉṟu avyāṉaippākaṉaik kaṇṭatum,
   aṭṭapaṅkaṉ cīṟi aḻaṉṟiṭṭavāṟum-aṭṭapaṅkaṉeṉṉum peyariṉaṉākiya avaṉ
   cīṟic ciṉantatum, tēvi iṭṭattiṟku-aracamātēviyākiya amirtamatiyiṉ
   viruppattukku, avaṉai iyaivittavāṟum-avaṉai yuṭaṉpaṭuvittatum, kūṭaṟku
   oṭṭiya kuṟiyum-avaṉaic ceṉṟu kūṭutaṟku niyamitta kālamum iṭamum,
   coṉṉāḷ-kuṇavati kūṟiṉāḷ, oḷḷiḻai-oḷḷiya iḻaiyaṇinta amutamati,
   uvappuṟṟāḷ-makiḻcci mikuntāḷ. e-ṟu.
   "naṉṟō navilka niṉkarumam" eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṭku nīṭṭiyātu viṭaiyiruppāḷ
   "paṭṭatu nalam" eṉṟāḷ; kāṇ, acai nilai. mā, acai. aḻalutal, vekuḷutal;
   iṭṭam, vaṭacoṟṟiripu. āṅku eṉpatu uraiyacai. kuṟiyiṉait tāṉē
   kuṟittāḷātaliṉ, aṭṭapaṅkaṟkēyaṉṟi amirtamatikkum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. uṟṟāḷ,
   uṟu eṉṉum uriccollaṭiyākap piṟanta muṟṟuviṉai.
   ----------
   amirtamati aracaṉpāl muṉivu koṇṭoḻukutal
   taṉivayi ṉikuḷai yāṉai tarappaṭu cāra ṉōṭu
       kaṉipurai kiḷavi kāmaṅ kalantaṉaḷ kaṉintu ceṉṟāḷ
       muṉiviṉai maṉṉaṉ ṟaṉmēṉ muṟukiṉa ḷoḻuku muṉpōl
       iṉiyava ḷalla ḷeṉko leṉamaṉat teṇṇi ṉāṉē.         116

   urai:- kaṉipurai kiḷavi-kaṉipōlum coṟkaḷaiyuṭaiya amutamati,
   ikuḷai-tōḻiyākiya kuṇavatipāl, tarappaṭu yāṉaiccāraṉōṭu-kūṭṭappaṭṭa
   yāṉaippākaṉākiya aṭṭapaṅkaṉuṭaṉ, taṉivayiṉ-taṉiyāṉa ōriṭattē,
   kāmaṅkaṉintu-kāmavēṭkai ciṟantu, kalantaṉaḷ
   ceṉṟāḷ-puṇarntuṭaṉoḻukivarupavaḷ, maṉṉaṉtaṉmēl- maṉṉaṉākiya
   yacōtaraṉpāl, muṉiviṉai muṟukiṉaḷ oḻukum-veṟuppumikak
   koṇṭoḻukaluṟṟāḷāka, muṉ pōl iṉiyavaḷ allaḷ eṉkol eṉa-muṉpōl ivaḷ nampāl
   iṉpuṟukiṉṟāḷillai itaṟkuk kāraṇam yātākum eṉṟu, maṉattu
   eṇṇiṉāṉ-yacōtaraṉ taṉ maṉattukkuḷḷē cintikkalāṉāṉ e-ṟu.
   taṉivayiṉ, tōḻi kuṟitta kuṟiyiṭam. ikuḷaitarappaṭu yāṉaic cāraṉōṭu
   taṉivayiṉ kāmaṅ kaṉintu kalantaṉaḷ ceṉṟāḷ eṉa iyaittukkoḷka.
   kāmaṅkalantaṉaḷ kaṉintuceṉṟāḷ eṉṟē koṇṭu kāmappuṇarcci peṟṟu
   ataṉkaṭkaṉṟi yoḻukupavaḷ eṉṟuraippiṉumām. cāraṉpāl maṉatteḻunta aṉpu
   ceṉṟamaiyiṉ, aracaṉpāl veṟuppu mikuvāḷāyiṉāḷeṉa vaṟika. ammikuti
   aracaṉuḷattē aiyattai yeḻuppiṉamaiyiṉ "muṉpōlaṉpila ḷeṉkol" eṉṟāṉ,
   itaṉaip piṟarpāl kūṟi yārāyātu taṉmaṉattakattē yeṇṇi ārāyat toṭaṅkiṉā
   ṉeṉpār "maṉatteṇṇiṉāṉ" eṉṟār.
   ----------
   maṉṉaṉ poyyuṟakkamkoṇṭu amutamatiyiṉ kaḷavoḻukkiṉaik kāṇṭal
   aracavai viṭuttu meyyā laṟuciṉa ṉoppa maṉṉaṉ
       uraiyala ṉamaḷi taṉmē luṟaṅkutal purinta pōḻtiṉ
       viraikamaḻ kuḻali mēvi meyttuyi leṉṟu kāmat
       tuṟaiyiṉaḷ peyarntu tōḻi kuṟiyiṭan tuṉṉi ṉāḷē.         117

   urai:- avai aracu viṭuttu- aracavaikkaṇ vantirunta aracarkaḷait tattam
   irukkaikkuc cellaviṭuttu, meyyāl aṟuciṉaṉ oppa-puṟattē maṉattuṭ ciṟitum
   ciṉam illāṉpōlak kāṭṭi, uraiyalaṉ-yāvarōṭum ētum collāṭaliṉṟi,
   amaḷitaṉmēl-taṉṉuṭaiya paṭukkaiyiṉmēl kiṭantu, maṉṉaṉ uṟaṅkutal purinta
   pōḻtu- maṉṉaṉākiya yacōtaraṉ poyyuṟakkaṅ koṇṭirukkaiyil, viraikamaḻ
   kuḻali-maṉam kamaḻum kūntalaiyuṭaiya amirtamati, mēvi-avaṉarukēpōntu,
   meyttuyil eṉṟu-meyyākavē uṟaṅkukiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu karutik koṇṭu,
   kāmattuṟaiyiṉaḷ-kāmaviṉpantuykkum kaḷavu neṟiyiṉaḷāy,
   peyarntu-paḷḷiyaṟaiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅki, tōḻi kuṟiiṭam tuṉṉiṉāḷ-tōḻikuṟitta
   kuṟiyiṭattai yaṭaintāḷ e-ṟu.
   amirtamatiyiṉ oḻukalāṟṟiṟ piṟanta aiyam nāḷukkunāḷ mikuntu yacōtaraṉ
   neñcai yalaittamaiyiṉ, aracavaikkaṇ iruntu ceyyum muṟaimaikkaṇ karuttuc
   cellāmaiyiṉ, "aracavai viṭuttu" eṉṟum, viṭṭāṉāyiṉum, piṟar ayirāmaip
   poruṭṭu, vekuḷiyaip puṟattē kāṭṭātoḻukiṉa ṉeṉṟaṟku, "meyyālaṟu
   ciṉaṉoppa" eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. aṟuciṉaṉ eṉpatu, "aruṅkēṭaṉ"1 eṉṟāṟ pōlvatu.
   poyyuṟakkaṅkoṇṭā ṉeṉpār. uṟaṅkiṉāṉeṉṉātu, "uṟaṅkutal purinta pōḻtu"
   eṉṟār; "purinta" eṉṟataṉāl, kāṇpavar meyyākavē uṟaṅku kiṉṟāṉeṉa uṇarat
   tōṟṟiṉā ṉeṉpatu peṟṟām. maṇaṅ kamaḻum kuḻaliyātaliṉ, amirtamati vantu
   tāṉ uṟaṅkutalaik kaṇṭēkuvataik kaṇṇālaṉṟi maṉattā laṟintukoṇṭamai
   tōṉṟa, "viraikamaḻ kuḻali" yeṉṟār. kaḷavuk kāmattuṟaiyil
   oḻukukiṉṟāḷātaliṉ, uṭaṉē avviṭattiṉiṉṟum nīṅkuvatu tōṉṟa, "peyarntu"
   eṉṟum, "kuṟiyiṭam tuṉṉiṉā"ḷeṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
